# Nordmark Exec — measure dictionary

> **DRAFT.** Model `pbi-report/Nordmark Exec/Nordmark Exec.SemanticModel`, table `_measures`. Generated by bip on 2026-10-28, regenerated
> 2026-10-30 for the period model of ADR-0013 (k) and the perf pass (SVG faces and pop-ups in one frame; 470 measures). Governed by ADR-0013 (Accepted 2026-10-28); requirement IDs from
> `docs/Requirements-exec.md`. Owner of every measure: bip. Tests: `pbi-report/Nordmark Exec/tests/control-totals.dax`
> (207 blocks) and `tests/web-sync.dax` (web sync 2026-11-16: answer key T-N11, card strings of 17 views T-N12, Explain texts T-N13, hunter's web oracle of 12 states T-N14).

## How the measures work

- **Window = dim_week rows in context.** Every base measure (folders 1-7) evaluates over the weeks in its filter context and the
  region picked in `param_region` (the leaf sums apply `dim_store[scope_code]`; ALL = no filter). The card layer (8) sets the window
  from the picked item: `[_Item Key]` reads the three picker slicers (`param_granularity` type, `dim_item[fy_label]`, `dim_item[label]`
  for Week / Month or `dim_item[label_wide]` for Quarter / Year) and falls back to the item of the picked type that holds the last pick
  (Reset = Week 43 FY2026). `dim_item` (calculated from `dim_week`) carries every bound: `[_Window Start]` .. `[_Window End]`, the LY
  window (`ly_week_key`, blank in FY2025), the previous item (`lw_week_key` at Week; else the item before, cut to the picked item's
  weeks when it is partial) and the EBITDA Previous window at Week (previous month's first k weeks, blank for week 5 of a 5-week month).
- **Flow vs point in time (E-93).** Flow metrics and channel mix sum over the window; stock, availability, GMROI and sell-through take
  the window's last week. The EBITDA card reads `[EBITDA Flash]`: month to date when the window is one week (Week items), else the window.
- **Status (E-22 .. E-26, E-95).** Badness per card in the rule unit, rounded to 10 decimals: On track if ≤ `watch_from`, Watch if ≤ `off_from`,
  else Off track (`param_status_rule`); the Watch edge is Watch (data README open point 9). Status is always vs budget / target
  (E-24), N/A where the value or its basis is blank (FY2025 has no budget); the dot uses the literal v5 rule over the cards with a
  status, N/A excluded (none rated = grey), and follows the item and the region.
- **Pickers.** `param_granularity` (filters `dim_item`), `param_comparator`, `param_region` (single select) and `param_attr_group / lever /
  owner` (multi select) are disconnected from the facts. Attribute picks only conceal cards (`[Card Revealed <KPI>]`).
- **Texts** follow `js-exec/lib/engine/format.ts` (minus U+2212, `€2,318m` from €1 bn up, `pp`, trimmed targets `97%`) and the
  sentences of `view.ts`; copy comes from `js-exec/lib/engine/copy.ts` (shared copy source).
- **SVG measures in one frame (perf pass 2026-10-30).** `Card SVG <KPI>`, `Info SVG <KPI>`, `Status Line SVG` and `Dot SVG` read the
  slicers once (picked `dim_item` row, type, comparator, region, attribute state), then build the face inside one `CALCULATE` that clears
  every picker table and passes the region as a `dim_store[scope_code]` filter, so the scoped leaf sums run their plain branch; every
  window `CALCULATE` is one VAR. Value, comparator, status and texts are computed in place with the same DAX as the `Card ... <KPI>`
  measures (which stay for the tests; T-N9 checks that both agree). A real dimension filter (e.g. `dim_category`) passes through.

## 1 Sales & margin

Flow metrics: sums over the dim_week rows in the filter context (the window).

| Measure | Definition | Spec | Format | Hidden |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Gross Sales` | Gross sales at ticket, ex-VAT. Flow: sums over the weeks in context. Scoped by the region switcher (dim_store.scope_code; ALL = no filter). | – | #,0.00 |  |
| `Markdown` | Markdown value, permanent + promo (E-34). Flow: sums over the weeks in context. Scoped by the region switcher (dim_store.scope_code; ALL = no filter). | E-34 | #,0.00 |  |
| `Returns` | Returns. Flow: sums over the weeks in context. Scoped by the region switcher (dim_store.scope_code; ALL = no filter). | – | #,0.00 |  |
| `Net Sales` | Net sales = gross - markdown - returns (E-30). Flow: sums over the weeks in context. Scoped by the region switcher (dim_store.scope_code; ALL = no filter). | E-30 | #,0.00 |  |
| `COGS` | Cost of goods at standard landed cost (E-32). Flow: sums over the weeks in context. Scoped by the region switcher (dim_store.scope_code; ALL = no filter). | E-32 | #,0.00 |  |
| `Back Margin` | Back margin: supplier income allocated to site x category by COGS share (E-33). Flow: sums over the weeks in context. Scoped by the region switcher (dim_store.scope_code; ALL = no filter). | E-33 | #,0.00 |  |
| `Front Margin` | Front margin EUR = Net sales - COGS (E-32). | E-32 | #,0.00 |  |
| `Total Margin` | Total margin EUR = front + back (E-33, ET-07). | E-33, ET-07 | #,0.00 |  |
| `Front GM %` | Front gross margin % of net sales (E-32). | E-32 | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |
| `Back Margin %` | Back margin % of net sales. | – | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |
| `Total Margin %` | Total margin % = (front + back) / net sales (E-33). | E-33 | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |
| `Markdown %` | Markdown % of gross sales (E-34). | E-34 | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |
| `Supplier Income` | Supplier income accrued (rebates + promo funding + listing fees). Equals Back Margin at ALL (ET-07). No site key. | ET-07 | #,0.00 |  |

<details><summary>DAX</summary>

**Gross Sales**

```dax
Gross Sales =
VAR _scope      = [_Scope Code]
RETURN
    IF (
        _scope = "ALL"
        ,SUM ( fact_sales_week[gross_sales] )
        ,CALCULATE ( SUM ( fact_sales_week[gross_sales] ), dim_store[scope_code] = _scope )
    )
```

**Markdown**

```dax
Markdown =
VAR _scope      = [_Scope Code]
RETURN
    IF (
        _scope = "ALL"
        ,SUM ( fact_sales_week[markdown_value] )
        ,CALCULATE ( SUM ( fact_sales_week[markdown_value] ), dim_store[scope_code] = _scope )
    )
```

**Returns**

```dax
Returns =
VAR _scope      = [_Scope Code]
RETURN
    IF (
        _scope = "ALL"
        ,SUM ( fact_sales_week[returns] )
        ,CALCULATE ( SUM ( fact_sales_week[returns] ), dim_store[scope_code] = _scope )
    )
```

**Net Sales**

```dax
Net Sales =
VAR _scope      = [_Scope Code]
RETURN
    IF (
        _scope = "ALL"
        ,SUM ( fact_sales_week[net_sales] )
        ,CALCULATE ( SUM ( fact_sales_week[net_sales] ), dim_store[scope_code] = _scope )
    )
```

**COGS**

```dax
COGS =
VAR _scope      = [_Scope Code]
RETURN
    IF (
        _scope = "ALL"
        ,SUM ( fact_sales_week[cogs_landed] )
        ,CALCULATE ( SUM ( fact_sales_week[cogs_landed] ), dim_store[scope_code] = _scope )
    )
```

**Back Margin**

```dax
Back Margin =
VAR _scope      = [_Scope Code]
RETURN
    IF (
        _scope = "ALL"
        ,SUM ( fact_sales_week[back_margin_alloc] )
        ,CALCULATE ( SUM ( fact_sales_week[back_margin_alloc] ), dim_store[scope_code] = _scope )
    )
```

**Front Margin**

```dax
Front Margin =
[Net Sales] - [COGS]
```

**Total Margin**

```dax
Total Margin =
[Front Margin] + [Back Margin]
```

**Front GM %**

```dax
Front GM % =
DIVIDE ( [Front Margin], [Net Sales] )
```

**Back Margin %**

```dax
Back Margin % =
DIVIDE ( [Back Margin], [Net Sales] )
```

**Total Margin %**

```dax
Total Margin % =
DIVIDE ( [Total Margin], [Net Sales] )
```

**Markdown %**

```dax
Markdown % =
DIVIDE ( [Markdown], [Gross Sales] )
```

**Supplier Income**

```dax
Supplier Income =
SUMX (
    fact_supplier_income_week
    ,fact_supplier_income_week[rebate_accrual] + fact_supplier_income_week[promo_funding] + fact_supplier_income_week[listing_fees]
)
```

</details>

## 2 Profit

OpEx by cost type (scenario ACT; the BUD twins sit in 6 Budget) and EBITDA. Central costs carry no site, so NL / BE / Online EBITDA is a contribution before central costs (data README open point 15).

| Measure | Definition | Spec | Format | Hidden |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Opex Shrink` | OpEx SHRINK, scenario ACT. Central costs carry no site and appear in ALL only (data README open point 15). BLANK under a category pick: OpEx has no category key (E-101). | E-101 | #,0.00 |  |
| `Opex Store` | OpEx STORE_OPEX, scenario ACT. Central costs carry no site and appear in ALL only (data README open point 15). BLANK under a category pick: OpEx has no category key (E-101). | E-101 | #,0.00 |  |
| `Opex Central` | OpEx CENTRAL_OPEX, scenario ACT. Central costs carry no site and appear in ALL only (data README open point 15). BLANK under a category pick: OpEx has no category key (E-101). | E-101 | #,0.00 |  |
| `Opex Fulfilment` | OpEx FULFILMENT, scenario ACT. Central costs carry no site and appear in ALL only (data README open point 15). BLANK under a category pick: OpEx has no category key (E-101). | E-101 | #,0.00 |  |
| `EBITDA` | EBITDA = total margin - shrink - store OpEx - central OpEx - fulfilment (E-40). In NL / BE / ONLINE a contribution before central costs. N/A under a category pick (E-101): Total margin - BLANK () would be Total margin, so the whole measure is blanked. | E-40, E-101 | #,0.00 |  |
| `EBITDA Margin %` | EBITDA % of net sales (E-40). | E-40 | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |
| `EBITDA PTD` | [EBITDA] for the period to date of the window's last week (the EBITDA card at Week, E-40, E-93). | E-40, E-93 | #,0.00 |  |
| `EBITDA Bud PTD` | [EBITDA Bud] for the period to date of the window's last week (the EBITDA card at Week, E-40, E-93). | E-40, E-93 | #,0.00 |  |
| `EBITDA Flash` | EBITDA card reading (E-40, E-93): [EBITDA PTD] (month to date through the week) when the window is one week (Week items, their LY week, the first week of a month), else [EBITDA] of the window (Month / Quarter / Year items and their comparators). | E-40, E-93 | #,0.00 |  |

<details><summary>DAX</summary>

**Opex Shrink**

```dax
Opex Shrink =
IF (
    [_Category Active]
    ,BLANK ()
    ,CALCULATE (
        [_Opex]
        ,fact_opex_week[scenario_code] = "ACT"
        ,fact_opex_week[cost_type_code] = "SHRINK"
    ) + 0
)
```

**Opex Store**

```dax
Opex Store =
IF (
    [_Category Active]
    ,BLANK ()
    ,CALCULATE (
        [_Opex]
        ,fact_opex_week[scenario_code] = "ACT"
        ,fact_opex_week[cost_type_code] = "STORE_OPEX"
    ) + 0
)
```

**Opex Central**

```dax
Opex Central =
IF (
    [_Category Active]
    ,BLANK ()
    ,CALCULATE (
        [_Opex]
        ,fact_opex_week[scenario_code] = "ACT"
        ,fact_opex_week[cost_type_code] = "CENTRAL_OPEX"
    ) + 0
)
```

**Opex Fulfilment**

```dax
Opex Fulfilment =
IF (
    [_Category Active]
    ,BLANK ()
    ,CALCULATE (
        [_Opex]
        ,fact_opex_week[scenario_code] = "ACT"
        ,fact_opex_week[cost_type_code] = "FULFILMENT"
    ) + 0
)
```

**EBITDA**

```dax
EBITDA =
IF (
    [_Category Active]
    ,BLANK ()
    ,[Total Margin] - [Opex Shrink] - [Opex Store] - [Opex Central] - [Opex Fulfilment]
)
```

**EBITDA Margin %**

```dax
EBITDA Margin % =
DIVIDE ( [EBITDA], [Net Sales] )
```

**EBITDA PTD**

```dax
EBITDA PTD =
VAR _last       = MAX ( dim_week[sort_index] )
VAR _period     =
    CALCULATE (
        SELECTEDVALUE ( dim_week[period_key] )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week )
        ,dim_week[sort_index] = _last
    )
RETURN
    CALCULATE (
        [EBITDA]
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week )
        ,dim_week[period_key] = _period
        ,dim_week[sort_index] <= _last
    )
```

**EBITDA Bud PTD**

```dax
EBITDA Bud PTD =
VAR _last       = MAX ( dim_week[sort_index] )
VAR _period     =
    CALCULATE (
        SELECTEDVALUE ( dim_week[period_key] )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week )
        ,dim_week[sort_index] = _last
    )
RETURN
    CALCULATE (
        [EBITDA Bud]
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week )
        ,dim_week[period_key] = _period
        ,dim_week[sort_index] <= _last
    )
```

**EBITDA Flash**

```dax
EBITDA Flash =
VAR _one        = COUNTROWS ( VALUES ( dim_week[sort_index] ) ) = 1
RETURN
    IF ( _one, [EBITDA PTD], [EBITDA] )
```

</details>

## 3 LFL

Like-for-like on the per-row flags is_lfl / is_lfl_as_ly; LY joins on ly_week_key, never on dates (E-12, ET-05).

| Measure | Definition | Spec | Format | Hidden |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `LFL Net Sales` | Net sales of rows whose store is LFL in that row's period (is_lfl, E-31). | E-31 | #,0.00 |  |
| `LFL Net Sales LY` | LY net sales of the stores that are LFL one year later, joined by ly_week_key, never by date (E-12, ET-05). | E-12, ET-05 | #,0.00 |  |
| `LFL Growth %` | LFL growth = LFL net sales / LY LFL net sales - 1; N/A without LY history (E-31). | E-31 | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |
| `LFL Net Sales Bud` | Budget net sales of the LFL stores. | – | #,0.00 |  |
| `LFL Growth % Bud` | Budget LFL = LFL budget net sales / the same LY actual - 1 (data README LFL). | – | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |
| `LFL Store Count` | Stores with is_lfl rows in the window's last week (131 at Week 43, ET-06). | ET-06 | #,0 |  |
| `Trading Store Count` | Stores with sales rows in the window's last week (142 at Week 43). | – | #,0 |  |
| `Store Net Sales` | Net sales of the store channel. | – | #,0.00 |  |
| `Store Net Sales LY` | LY net sales of the store channel by ly_week_key. | – | #,0.00 |  |
| `Non-LFL Effect pp` | Store-channel growth vs LY - LFL growth (ET-06). | ET-06 | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |
| `Online Growth vs LY` | Online net sales vs LY by week key (LFL card stat). | – | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |

<details><summary>DAX</summary>

**LFL Net Sales**

```dax
LFL Net Sales =
CALCULATE ( [Net Sales], fact_sales_week[is_lfl] = TRUE () )
```

**LFL Net Sales LY**

```dax
LFL Net Sales LY =
VAR _ly         = VALUES ( dim_week[ly_week_key] )
RETURN
    CALCULATE (
        [Net Sales]
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week )
        ,TREATAS ( _ly, dim_week[week_key] )
        ,fact_sales_week[is_lfl_as_ly] = TRUE ()
    )
```

**LFL Growth %**

```dax
LFL Growth % =
VAR _ly         = [LFL Net Sales LY]
RETURN
    IF ( _ly <> 0, DIVIDE ( [LFL Net Sales], _ly ) - 1 )
```

**LFL Net Sales Bud**

```dax
LFL Net Sales Bud =
IF (
    [LFL Net Sales LY] <> 0
    ,CALCULATE ( [Net Sales Bud], fact_budget_week[is_lfl] = TRUE () )
)
```

**LFL Growth % Bud**

```dax
LFL Growth % Bud =
VAR _ly         = [LFL Net Sales LY]
VAR _bud        = [LFL Net Sales Bud]
RETURN
    IF ( _ly <> 0 && NOT ISBLANK ( _bud ), DIVIDE ( _bud, _ly ) - 1 )
```

**LFL Store Count**

```dax
LFL Store Count =
VAR _last       = MAX ( dim_week[sort_index] )
RETURN
    IF (
        NOT ISBLANK ( [LFL Growth %] )
        ,CALCULATE (
            [_Store Count]
            ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week )
            ,dim_week[sort_index] = _last
            ,fact_sales_week[is_lfl] = TRUE ()
            ,dim_store[channel_code] = "STORE"
        ) + 0
    )
```

**Trading Store Count**

```dax
Trading Store Count =
VAR _last       = MAX ( dim_week[sort_index] )
RETURN
    CALCULATE (
        [_Store Count]
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week )
        ,dim_week[sort_index] = _last
        ,dim_store[channel_code] = "STORE"
    )
```

**Store Net Sales**

```dax
Store Net Sales =
CALCULATE ( [Net Sales], dim_store[channel_code] = "STORE" ) + 0
```

**Store Net Sales LY**

```dax
Store Net Sales LY =
VAR _ly         = VALUES ( dim_week[ly_week_key] )
RETURN
    CALCULATE (
        [Net Sales]
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week )
        ,TREATAS ( _ly, dim_week[week_key] )
        ,dim_store[channel_code] = "STORE"
    ) + 0
```

**Non-LFL Effect pp**

```dax
Non-LFL Effect pp =
VAR _lfl        = [LFL Growth %]
RETURN
    IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _lfl ), DIVIDE ( [Store Net Sales], [Store Net Sales LY] ) - 1 - _lfl )
```

**Online Growth vs LY**

```dax
Online Growth vs LY =
VAR _ly         = VALUES ( dim_week[ly_week_key] )
VAR _cur        = CALCULATE ( [Net Sales], dim_store[channel_code] = "ONLINE" )
VAR _prev       =
    CALCULATE (
        [Net Sales]
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week )
        ,TREATAS ( _ly, dim_week[week_key] )
        ,dim_store[channel_code] = "ONLINE"
    )
RETURN
    IF ( _prev <> 0, DIVIDE ( _cur, _prev ) - 1 )
```

</details>

## 4 Stock & supply

Point-in-time metrics take the window's last week-end; GMROI and sell-through build their own 52-week / season window from it.

| Measure | Definition | Spec | Format | Hidden |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `On Hand Cost` | Stock on hand at cost. Point in time: the window's last week-end (E-13). | E-13 | #,0.00 |  |
| `On Order Cost` | Stock on order at cost. Point in time: the window's last week-end (E-13). | E-13 | #,0.00 |  |
| `Stock incl On Order` | On hand + on order at cost (E-35). | E-35 | #,0.00 |  |
| `Forecast COGS` | Forecast weekly COGS of the window's last week. Point in time: the window's last week-end (E-13). | E-13 | #,0.00 |  |
| `Weeks of Cover` | (On hand + on order) / forecast weekly COGS at the last week-end; fallback R4W average COGS when the forecast is blank (E-35, ET-08). | E-35, ET-08 | 0.0 |  |
| `Cover Target Wks` | Forecast-COGS-weighted category cover target, same week and scope (9.5 at ALL, ET-08). | ET-08 | 0.0 |  |
| `Ranged SKU Days` | Ranged top-500 SKU-days (stores only). Point in time: the window's last week-end (E-13). | E-13 | #,0 |  |
| `In Stock SKU Days` | In-stock SKU-days. Point in time: the window's last week-end (E-13). | E-13 | #,0.0 |  |
| `Availability %` | In-stock / ranged SKU-days, last week; N/A for ONLINE (E-36). | E-36 | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |
| `Availability Target` | Availability target, param_target ALL (97 %). | – | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |
| `Season Week` | Season week of the window's last week (AW26 = W32-W51; blank outside a season, E-16). | E-16 | 0 |  |
| `Season Opening Units` | Seasonal on-hand units at the week-end before season week 1, selling locations (E-37); the seasonal part of a category pick, BLANK without one (E-101). | E-37, E-101 | #,0 |  |
| `Season Receipt Units` | Seasonal receipts season to date (E-37); the seasonal part of a category pick, BLANK without one (E-101). | E-37, E-101 | #,0 |  |
| `Season Units Sold` | Seasonal units sold season to date, stores and ONLINE (wholesale excluded, E-37); the seasonal part of a category pick, BLANK without one (E-101). | E-37, E-101 | #,0 |  |
| `Sell-through %` | Sold / (opening + receipts), season to date; N/A outside a season (E-37; bars 12 -> 63 %, data README open point 13). | E-37 | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |
| `Sell-through Target` | Season sell-through target (62 %; every seasonal category row is 0.62, so the (opening + receipts)-weighted mean over a pick is 0.62 too); BLANK without a seasonal category in context (E-101, E-102). | E-101, E-102 | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |
| `GMROI Front Margin R52W` | Front margin over the 52 weeks ending with the window's last week (E-38, OQ 11). | E-38 | #,0.00 |  |
| `GMROI Avg On Hand Cost` | Average on-hand cost over the 52 week-ends (on-order and in-transit excluded, E-38). | E-38 | #,0.00 |  |
| `GMROI` | GMROI = R52W front margin / average on-hand cost (E-38). | E-38 | 0.00 |  |
| `GMROI Target` | GMROI target: 2.5x at All (param_target ALL); under a category pick the average-on-hand-cost-weighted mean of dim_category.gmroi_target over the pick, i.e. the category's own row for a single pick (E-102, ruling 2026-10-30). | E-102 | 0.0 |  |
| `PO Lines Due` | PO lines due. No site key: the same in every scope. No category key: BLANK under a category pick (E-101). | E-101 | #,0 |  |
| `PO Lines OTIF` | PO lines on time in full; BLANK under a category pick (E-101). | E-101 | #,0 |  |
| `OTIF %` | Supplier OTIF % over the window (E-39). | E-39 | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |
| `OTIF Target` | OTIF target 95 %; BLANK under a category pick (E-101). | E-101 | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |
| `OTIF Suppliers Below 85` | Suppliers below 85 % OTIF in the window's last week (OTIF card stat); BLANK under a category pick (E-101). | E-101 | #,0 |  |

<details><summary>DAX</summary>

**On Hand Cost**

```dax
On Hand Cost =
VAR _last       = MAX ( dim_week[sort_index] )
RETURN
    CALCULATE (
        [_Inv On Hand Cost]
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week )
        ,dim_week[sort_index] = _last
    )
```

**On Order Cost**

```dax
On Order Cost =
VAR _last       = MAX ( dim_week[sort_index] )
RETURN
    CALCULATE (
        [_Inv On Order Cost]
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week )
        ,dim_week[sort_index] = _last
    )
```

**Stock incl On Order**

```dax
Stock incl On Order =
[On Hand Cost] + [On Order Cost]
```

**Forecast COGS**

```dax
Forecast COGS =
VAR _last       = MAX ( dim_week[sort_index] )
RETURN
    CALCULATE (
        [_Forecast COGS]
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week )
        ,dim_week[sort_index] = _last
    )
```

**Weeks of Cover**

```dax
Weeks of Cover =
VAR _last       = MAX ( dim_week[sort_index] )
VAR _forecast   = [Forecast COGS]
VAR _stock      = [Stock incl On Order]
VAR _r4w        =
    DIVIDE (
        CALCULATE (
            [COGS]
            ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week )
            ,dim_week[sort_index] > _last - 4
            ,dim_week[sort_index] <= _last
        )
        ,4
    )
RETURN
    IF ( _forecast <> 0, DIVIDE ( _stock, _forecast ), DIVIDE ( _stock, _r4w ) )
```

**Cover Target Wks**

```dax
Cover Target Wks =
VAR _last       = MAX ( dim_week[sort_index] )
RETURN
    DIVIDE (
        CALCULATE ( [_Forecast x Cover Target], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _last )
        ,CALCULATE ( [_Forecast COGS], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _last )
    )
```

**Ranged SKU Days**

```dax
Ranged SKU Days =
VAR _last       = MAX ( dim_week[sort_index] )
RETURN
    CALCULATE (
        [_Ranged SKU Days]
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week )
        ,dim_week[sort_index] = _last
    )
```

**In Stock SKU Days**

```dax
In Stock SKU Days =
VAR _last       = MAX ( dim_week[sort_index] )
RETURN
    CALCULATE (
        [_In Stock SKU Days]
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week )
        ,dim_week[sort_index] = _last
    )
```

**Availability %**

```dax
Availability % =
VAR _ranged     = [Ranged SKU Days]
RETURN
    IF ( _ranged <> 0, DIVIDE ( [In Stock SKU Days], _ranged ) )
```

**Availability Target**

```dax
Availability Target =
LOOKUPVALUE ( param_target[target_value], param_target[kpi_code], "AVAILABILITY", param_target[category_code], "ALL" )
```

**Season Week**

```dax
Season Week =
VAR _last       = MAX ( dim_week[sort_index] )
RETURN
    CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[season_week] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _last )
```

**Season Opening Units**

```dax
Season Opening Units =
VAR _last       = MAX ( dim_week[sort_index] )
VAR _sw         = [Season Week]
RETURN
    IF (
        NOT ISBLANK ( _sw ) && TRUE () IN VALUES ( dim_category[is_seasonal] )
        ,CALCULATE (
            [_Inv On Hand Units]
            ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week )
            ,dim_week[sort_index] = _last - _sw
            ,dim_store[channel_code] IN { "STORE", "ONLINE" }
            ,dim_category[is_seasonal] = TRUE ()
        ) + 0
    )
```

**Season Receipt Units**

```dax
Season Receipt Units =
VAR _last       = MAX ( dim_week[sort_index] )
VAR _sw         = [Season Week]
RETURN
    IF (
        NOT ISBLANK ( _sw ) && TRUE () IN VALUES ( dim_category[is_seasonal] )
        ,CALCULATE (
            [_Receipt Units]
            ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week )
            ,dim_week[sort_index] > _last - _sw
            ,dim_week[sort_index] <= _last
            ,dim_category[is_seasonal] = TRUE ()
        ) + 0
    )
```

**Season Units Sold**

```dax
Season Units Sold =
VAR _last       = MAX ( dim_week[sort_index] )
VAR _sw         = [Season Week]
RETURN
    IF (
        NOT ISBLANK ( _sw ) && TRUE () IN VALUES ( dim_category[is_seasonal] )
        ,CALCULATE (
            [_Units]
            ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week )
            ,dim_week[sort_index] > _last - _sw
            ,dim_week[sort_index] <= _last
            ,dim_store[channel_code] IN { "STORE", "ONLINE" }
            ,dim_category[is_seasonal] = TRUE ()
        ) + 0
    )
```

**Sell-through %**

```dax
Sell-through % =
VAR _sw         = [Season Week]
RETURN
    IF (
        NOT ISBLANK ( _sw )
        ,DIVIDE ( [Season Units Sold], [Season Opening Units] + [Season Receipt Units] )
    )
```

**Sell-through Target**

```dax
Sell-through Target =
IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( [Season Week] ) && TRUE () IN VALUES ( dim_category[is_seasonal] ), LOOKUPVALUE ( param_target[target_value], param_target[kpi_code], "SELL_THROUGH", param_target[category_code], "ALL" ) )
```

**GMROI Front Margin R52W**

```dax
GMROI Front Margin R52W =
VAR _last       = MAX ( dim_week[sort_index] )
RETURN
    IF (
        _last >= 52
        ,CALCULATE (
            [Front Margin]
            ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week )
            ,dim_week[sort_index] > _last - 52
            ,dim_week[sort_index] <= _last
        )
    )
```

**GMROI Avg On Hand Cost**

```dax
GMROI Avg On Hand Cost =
VAR _last       = MAX ( dim_week[sort_index] )
RETURN
    IF (
        _last >= 52
        ,DIVIDE (
            CALCULATE (
                [_Inv On Hand Cost]
                ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week )
                ,dim_week[sort_index] > _last - 52
                ,dim_week[sort_index] <= _last
            )
            ,52
        )
    )
```

**GMROI**

```dax
GMROI =
DIVIDE ( [GMROI Front Margin R52W], [GMROI Avg On Hand Cost] )
```

**GMROI Target**

```dax
GMROI Target =
IF (
    MAX ( dim_week[sort_index] ) >= 52
    ,IF (
        [_Category Active]
        ,DIVIDE (
            SUMX (
                VALUES ( dim_category[category_code] )
                ,[GMROI Avg On Hand Cost] * CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_category[gmroi_target] ) )
            )
            ,[GMROI Avg On Hand Cost]
        )
        ,LOOKUPVALUE ( param_target[target_value], param_target[kpi_code], "GMROI", param_target[category_code], "ALL" )
    )
)
```

**PO Lines Due**

```dax
PO Lines Due =
IF ( [_Category Active], BLANK (), SUM ( fact_po_week[po_lines_due] ) )
```

**PO Lines OTIF**

```dax
PO Lines OTIF =
IF ( [_Category Active], BLANK (), SUM ( fact_po_week[po_lines_otif] ) )
```

**OTIF %**

```dax
OTIF % =
DIVIDE ( [PO Lines OTIF], [PO Lines Due] )
```

**OTIF Target**

```dax
OTIF Target =
IF ( [_Category Active], BLANK (), LOOKUPVALUE ( param_target[target_value], param_target[kpi_code], "SUPPLIER_OTIF", param_target[category_code], "ALL" ) )
```

**OTIF Suppliers Below 85**

```dax
OTIF Suppliers Below 85 =
VAR _last       = MAX ( dim_week[sort_index] )
RETURN
    IF (
        NOT [_Category Active]
        ,COUNTROWS (
            FILTER (
                CALCULATETABLE ( fact_po_week, REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _last )
                ,fact_po_week[po_lines_otif] < 0.85 * fact_po_week[po_lines_due]
            )
        ) + 0
    )
```

</details>

## 5 Channel mix

Shares over the whole window, plan = budget shares over the same weeks (E-41, E-93; USER 2026-10-29; at Week the week itself).

| Measure | Definition | Spec | Format | Hidden |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Mix Store Net Sales` | Store channel net sales over the window (E-41). | E-41 | #,0.00 |  |
| `Mix Store %` | Store share of net sales over the whole window (E-41; at Week the week itself). | E-41 | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |
| `Mix Store % Bud` | Plan store share = budget net-sales share over the same window (E-41); blank without budget (FY2025). | E-41 | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |
| `Mix Online Net Sales` | Online channel net sales over the window (E-41). | E-41 | #,0.00 |  |
| `Mix Online %` | Online share of net sales over the whole window (E-41; at Week the week itself). | E-41 | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |
| `Mix Online % Bud` | Plan online share = budget net-sales share over the same window (E-41); blank without budget (FY2025). | E-41 | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |
| `Mix Wholesale Net Sales` | Wholesale channel net sales over the window (E-41). | E-41 | #,0.00 |  |
| `Mix Wholesale %` | Wholesale share of net sales over the whole window (E-41; at Week the week itself). | E-41 | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |
| `Mix Wholesale % Bud` | Plan wholesale share = budget net-sales share over the same window (E-41); blank without budget (FY2025). | E-41 | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |
| `Mix Max Dev pp` | Channel with the largest absolute deviation from the plan mix (1 store, 2 online, 3 wholesale; first on a tie). pp is signed. | – | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |
| `Mix Max Dev Channel` | Channel with the largest absolute deviation from the plan mix (1 store, 2 online, 3 wholesale; first on a tie). pp is signed. | – | 0 |  |

<details><summary>DAX</summary>

**Mix Store Net Sales**

```dax
Mix Store Net Sales =
CALCULATE ( [Net Sales], dim_store[channel_code] = "STORE" ) + 0
```

**Mix Store %**

```dax
Mix Store % =
DIVIDE ( [Mix Store Net Sales], [Net Sales] )
```

**Mix Store % Bud**

```dax
Mix Store % Bud =
VAR _total      = [Net Sales Bud]
RETURN
    IF (
        NOT ISBLANK ( _total )
        ,DIVIDE ( CALCULATE ( [Net Sales Bud], dim_store[channel_code] = "STORE" ) + 0, _total )
    )
```

**Mix Online Net Sales**

```dax
Mix Online Net Sales =
CALCULATE ( [Net Sales], dim_store[channel_code] = "ONLINE" ) + 0
```

**Mix Online %**

```dax
Mix Online % =
DIVIDE ( [Mix Online Net Sales], [Net Sales] )
```

**Mix Online % Bud**

```dax
Mix Online % Bud =
VAR _total      = [Net Sales Bud]
RETURN
    IF (
        NOT ISBLANK ( _total )
        ,DIVIDE ( CALCULATE ( [Net Sales Bud], dim_store[channel_code] = "ONLINE" ) + 0, _total )
    )
```

**Mix Wholesale Net Sales**

```dax
Mix Wholesale Net Sales =
CALCULATE ( [Net Sales], dim_store[channel_code] = "WHOLESALE" ) + 0
```

**Mix Wholesale %**

```dax
Mix Wholesale % =
DIVIDE ( [Mix Wholesale Net Sales], [Net Sales] )
```

**Mix Wholesale % Bud**

```dax
Mix Wholesale % Bud =
VAR _total      = [Net Sales Bud]
RETURN
    IF (
        NOT ISBLANK ( _total )
        ,DIVIDE ( CALCULATE ( [Net Sales Bud], dim_store[channel_code] = "WHOLESALE" ) + 0, _total )
    )
```

**Mix Max Dev pp**

```dax
Mix Max Dev pp =
VAR _b1         = [Mix Store % Bud]
VAR _d1         = [Mix Store %] - _b1
VAR _d2         = [Mix Online %] - [Mix Online % Bud]
VAR _d3         = [Mix Wholesale %] - [Mix Wholesale % Bud]
VAR _j          =
    SWITCH (
        TRUE ()
        ,ABS ( _d1 ) >= ABS ( _d2 ) && ABS ( _d1 ) >= ABS ( _d3 )
            ,1
        ,ABS ( _d2 ) >= ABS ( _d3 )
            ,2
        ,3
    )
RETURN
    IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _b1 ), SWITCH ( _j, 1, _d1, 2, _d2, _d3 ) )
```

**Mix Max Dev Channel**

```dax
Mix Max Dev Channel =
VAR _b1         = [Mix Store % Bud]
VAR _d1         = [Mix Store %] - _b1
VAR _d2         = [Mix Online %] - [Mix Online % Bud]
VAR _d3         = [Mix Wholesale %] - [Mix Wholesale % Bud]
VAR _j          =
    SWITCH (
        TRUE ()
        ,ABS ( _d1 ) >= ABS ( _d2 ) && ABS ( _d1 ) >= ABS ( _d3 )
            ,1
        ,ABS ( _d2 ) >= ABS ( _d3 )
            ,2
        ,3
    )
RETURN
    IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _b1 ), _j )
```

</details>

## 6 Budget

fact_budget_week and fact_opex_week scenario BUD (FY2026 only: every budget-based value is blank, so N/A, in FY2025, E-95).

| Measure | Definition | Spec | Format | Hidden |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Opex Shrink Bud` | OpEx SHRINK, scenario BUD. Central costs carry no site and appear in ALL only (data README open point 15). BLANK under a category pick: OpEx has no category key (E-101). | E-101 | #,0.00 |  |
| `Opex Store Bud` | OpEx STORE_OPEX, scenario BUD. Central costs carry no site and appear in ALL only (data README open point 15). BLANK under a category pick: OpEx has no category key (E-101). | E-101 | #,0.00 |  |
| `Opex Central Bud` | OpEx CENTRAL_OPEX, scenario BUD. Central costs carry no site and appear in ALL only (data README open point 15). BLANK under a category pick: OpEx has no category key (E-101). | E-101 | #,0.00 |  |
| `Opex Fulfilment Bud` | OpEx FULFILMENT, scenario BUD. Central costs carry no site and appear in ALL only (data README open point 15). BLANK under a category pick: OpEx has no category key (E-101). | E-101 | #,0.00 |  |
| `EBITDA Flash Bud` | EBITDA card reading (E-40, E-93): [EBITDA Bud PTD] (month to date through the week) when the window is one week (Week items, their LY week, the first week of a month), else [EBITDA Bud] of the window (Month / Quarter / Year items and their comparators). | E-40, E-93 | #,0.00 |  |
| `Net Sales Bud` | Budget net_sales_bud (FY2026 W1-W52). Scoped by the region switcher (dim_store.scope_code; ALL = no filter). | – | #,0.00 |  |
| `Gross Sales Bud` | Budget gross_sales_bud (FY2026 W1-W52). Scoped by the region switcher (dim_store.scope_code; ALL = no filter). | – | #,0.00 |  |
| `Markdown Bud` | Budget markdown_bud (FY2026 W1-W52). Scoped by the region switcher (dim_store.scope_code; ALL = no filter). | – | #,0.00 |  |
| `COGS Bud` | Budget cogs_bud (FY2026 W1-W52). Scoped by the region switcher (dim_store.scope_code; ALL = no filter). | – | #,0.00 |  |
| `Back Margin Bud` | Budget back_margin_bud (FY2026 W1-W52). Scoped by the region switcher (dim_store.scope_code; ALL = no filter). | – | #,0.00 |  |
| `Markdown % Bud` | Budget markdown % of gross. | – | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |
| `Front GM % Bud` | Budget front GM % = 1 - COGS bud / net sales bud. | – | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |
| `Total Margin % Bud` | Budget total margin %. | – | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |
| `EBITDA Bud` | Budget EBITDA = budget total margin - budget OpEx (scenario BUD); N/A under a category pick (E-101). | E-101 | #,0.00 |  |
| `EBITDA Margin % Bud` | Budget EBITDA % of budget net sales. | – | 0.0%;-0.0%;0.0% |  |

<details><summary>DAX</summary>

**Opex Shrink Bud**

```dax
Opex Shrink Bud =
IF (
    [_Category Active]
    ,BLANK ()
    ,CALCULATE (
        [_Opex]
        ,fact_opex_week[scenario_code] = "BUD"
        ,fact_opex_week[cost_type_code] = "SHRINK"
    ) + 0
)
```

**Opex Store Bud**

```dax
Opex Store Bud =
IF (
    [_Category Active]
    ,BLANK ()
    ,CALCULATE (
        [_Opex]
        ,fact_opex_week[scenario_code] = "BUD"
        ,fact_opex_week[cost_type_code] = "STORE_OPEX"
    ) + 0
)
```

**Opex Central Bud**

```dax
Opex Central Bud =
IF (
    [_Category Active]
    ,BLANK ()
    ,CALCULATE (
        [_Opex]
        ,fact_opex_week[scenario_code] = "BUD"
        ,fact_opex_week[cost_type_code] = "CENTRAL_OPEX"
    ) + 0
)
```

**Opex Fulfilment Bud**

```dax
Opex Fulfilment Bud =
IF (
    [_Category Active]
    ,BLANK ()
    ,CALCULATE (
        [_Opex]
        ,fact_opex_week[scenario_code] = "BUD"
        ,fact_opex_week[cost_type_code] = "FULFILMENT"
    ) + 0
)
```

**EBITDA Flash Bud**

```dax
EBITDA Flash Bud =
VAR _one        = COUNTROWS ( VALUES ( dim_week[sort_index] ) ) = 1
RETURN
    IF ( _one, [EBITDA Bud PTD], [EBITDA Bud] )
```

**Net Sales Bud**

```dax
Net Sales Bud =
VAR _scope      = [_Scope Code]
RETURN
    IF (
        _scope = "ALL"
        ,SUM ( fact_budget_week[net_sales_bud] )
        ,CALCULATE ( SUM ( fact_budget_week[net_sales_bud] ), dim_store[scope_code] = _scope )
    )
```

**Gross Sales Bud**

```dax
Gross Sales Bud =
VAR _scope      = [_Scope Code]
RETURN
    IF (
        _scope = "ALL"
        ,SUM ( fact_budget_week[gross_sales_bud] )
        ,CALCULATE ( SUM ( fact_budget_week[gross_sales_bud] ), dim_store[scope_code] = _scope )
    )
```

**Markdown Bud**

```dax
Markdown Bud =
VAR _scope      = [_Scope Code]
RETURN
    IF (
        _scope = "ALL"
        ,SUM ( fact_budget_week[markdown_bud] )
        ,CALCULATE ( SUM ( fact_budget_week[markdown_bud] ), dim_store[scope_code] = _scope )
    )
```

**COGS Bud**

```dax
COGS Bud =
VAR _scope      = [_Scope Code]
RETURN
    IF (
        _scope = "ALL"
        ,SUM ( fact_budget_week[cogs_bud] )
        ,CALCULATE ( SUM ( fact_budget_week[cogs_bud] ), dim_store[scope_code] = _scope )
    )
```

**Back Margin Bud**

```dax
Back Margin Bud =
VAR _scope      = [_Scope Code]
RETURN
    IF (
        _scope = "ALL"
        ,SUM ( fact_budget_week[back_margin_bud] )
        ,CALCULATE ( SUM ( fact_budget_week[back_margin_bud] ), dim_store[scope_code] = _scope )
    )
```

**Markdown % Bud**

```dax
Markdown % Bud =
DIVIDE ( [Markdown Bud], [Gross Sales Bud] )
```

**Front GM % Bud**

```dax
Front GM % Bud =
VAR _ns         = [Net Sales Bud]
RETURN
    IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _ns ), 1 - DIVIDE ( [COGS Bud], _ns ) )
```

**Total Margin % Bud**

```dax
Total Margin % Bud =
DIVIDE ( [Net Sales Bud] - [COGS Bud] + [Back Margin Bud], [Net Sales Bud] )
```

**EBITDA Bud**

```dax
EBITDA Bud =
VAR _ns         = [Net Sales Bud]
RETURN
    IF (
        NOT ISBLANK ( _ns ) && NOT [_Category Active]
        ,_ns - [COGS Bud] + [Back Margin Bud] - [Opex Shrink Bud] - [Opex Store Bud] - [Opex Central Bud] - [Opex Fulfilment Bud]
    )
```

**EBITDA Margin % Bud**

```dax
EBITDA Margin % Bud =
DIVIDE ( [EBITDA Bud], [Net Sales Bud] )
```

</details>

## 7 Status

Status codes 1 On track / 2 Watch / 3 Off track (blank = N/A) in the window context, always vs budget / target; counts and the overall dot over the cards with a status.

| Measure | Definition | Spec | Format | Hidden |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Status NET_SALES` | Status of NET_SALES: 1 On track, 2 Watch, 3 Off track, blank N/A. Always vs budget / target (E-22, E-24); N/A where the value or its basis is blank (FY2025 has no budget, E-95); badness rounded to 10 decimals, the Watch edge is Watch (data README Status, open point 9). | E-22, E-24, E-95 | 0 |  |
| `Status TOTAL_MARGIN` | Status of TOTAL_MARGIN: 1 On track, 2 Watch, 3 Off track, blank N/A. Always vs budget / target (E-22, E-24); N/A where the value or its basis is blank (FY2025 has no budget, E-95); badness rounded to 10 decimals, the Watch edge is Watch (data README Status, open point 9). | E-22, E-24, E-95 | 0 |  |
| `Status WEEKS_OF_COVER` | Status of WEEKS_OF_COVER: 1 On track, 2 Watch, 3 Off track, blank N/A. Always vs budget / target (E-22, E-24); N/A where the value or its basis is blank (FY2025 has no budget, E-95); badness rounded to 10 decimals, the Watch edge is Watch (data README Status, open point 9). | E-22, E-24, E-95 | 0 |  |
| `Status MARKDOWN` | Status of MARKDOWN: 1 On track, 2 Watch, 3 Off track, blank N/A. Always vs budget / target (E-22, E-24); N/A where the value or its basis is blank (FY2025 has no budget, E-95); badness rounded to 10 decimals, the Watch edge is Watch (data README Status, open point 9). | E-22, E-24, E-95 | 0 |  |
| `Status AVAILABILITY` | Status of AVAILABILITY: 1 On track, 2 Watch, 3 Off track, blank N/A. Always vs budget / target (E-22, E-24); N/A where the value or its basis is blank (FY2025 has no budget, E-95); badness rounded to 10 decimals, the Watch edge is Watch (data README Status, open point 9). | E-22, E-24, E-95 | 0 |  |
| `Status EBITDA` | Status of EBITDA: 1 On track, 2 Watch, 3 Off track, blank N/A. Always vs budget / target (E-22, E-24); N/A where the value or its basis is blank (FY2025 has no budget, E-95); badness rounded to 10 decimals, the Watch edge is Watch (data README Status, open point 9). | E-22, E-24, E-95 | 0 |  |
| `Status LFL_GROWTH` | Status of LFL_GROWTH: 1 On track, 2 Watch, 3 Off track, blank N/A. Always vs budget / target (E-22, E-24); N/A where the value or its basis is blank (FY2025 has no budget, E-95); badness rounded to 10 decimals, the Watch edge is Watch (data README Status, open point 9). | E-22, E-24, E-95 | 0 |  |
| `Status FRONT_GM` | Status of FRONT_GM: 1 On track, 2 Watch, 3 Off track, blank N/A. Always vs budget / target (E-22, E-24); N/A where the value or its basis is blank (FY2025 has no budget, E-95); badness rounded to 10 decimals, the Watch edge is Watch (data README Status, open point 9). | E-22, E-24, E-95 | 0 |  |
| `Status SELL_THROUGH` | Status of SELL_THROUGH: 1 On track, 2 Watch, 3 Off track, blank N/A. Always vs budget / target (E-22, E-24); N/A where the value or its basis is blank (FY2025 has no budget, E-95); badness rounded to 10 decimals, the Watch edge is Watch (data README Status, open point 9). | E-22, E-24, E-95 | 0 |  |
| `Status GMROI` | Status of GMROI: 1 On track, 2 Watch, 3 Off track, blank N/A. Always vs budget / target (E-22, E-24); N/A where the value or its basis is blank (FY2025 has no budget, E-95); badness rounded to 10 decimals, the Watch edge is Watch (data README Status, open point 9). | E-22, E-24, E-95 | 0 |  |
| `Status SUPPLIER_OTIF` | Status of SUPPLIER_OTIF: 1 On track, 2 Watch, 3 Off track, blank N/A. Always vs budget / target (E-22, E-24); N/A where the value or its basis is blank (FY2025 has no budget, E-95); badness rounded to 10 decimals, the Watch edge is Watch (data README Status, open point 9). | E-22, E-24, E-95 | 0 |  |
| `Status CHANNEL_MIX` | Status of CHANNEL_MIX: 1 On track, 2 Watch, 3 Off track, blank N/A. Always vs budget / target (E-22, E-24); N/A where the value or its basis is blank (FY2025 has no budget, E-95); badness rounded to 10 decimals, the Watch edge is Watch (data README Status, open point 9). | E-22, E-24, E-95 | 0 |  |
| `Status Count On` | Cards On track (E-25), over the cards with a status (E-95). | E-25, E-95 | 0 |  |
| `Status Count Watch` | Cards Watch (E-25), over the cards with a status (E-95). | E-25, E-95 | 0 |  |
| `Status Count Off` | Cards Off track (E-25), over the cards with a status (E-95). | E-25, E-95 | 0 |  |
| `Status Count NA` | Cards without a status (N/A). | – | 0 |  |
| `Status Dot` | Overall dot: 1 green, 2 orange, 3 red. Literal v5 rule over the cards with a status (N/A excluded), constants from param_status_rule (E-26, ADR-0013 (i)); follows period and region, ignores attribute filters. | E-26 | 0 |  |
| `Status Pack` | On / watch / off counts and the dot in one pass over the 12 statuses (on x 1000 + watch x 100 + off x 10 + dot; dot 0 = no rated card, grey, E-95); the status line and the dot tooltip read it. | E-95 | 0 | yes |
| `Status Summary Text` | Status-line summary: revealed cards of 12 and the attribute picks (web status strip). | – | – |  |

<details><summary>DAX</summary>

**Status NET_SALES**

```dax
Status NET_SALES =
VAR _bud        = [Net Sales Bud]
VAR _bad        = IF ( _bud <> 0, 1 - DIVIDE ( [Net Sales], _bud ) )
VAR _watch      = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[watch_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "NET_SALES" )
VAR _off        = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[off_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "NET_SALES" )
VAR _r          = ROUND ( _bad, 10 )
RETURN
    IF (
        NOT ISBLANK ( _bad )
        ,SWITCH (
            TRUE ()
            ,_r <= _watch
                ,1
            ,_r <= _off
                ,2
            ,3
        )
    )
```

**Status TOTAL_MARGIN**

```dax
Status TOTAL_MARGIN =
VAR _act        = [Total Margin %]
VAR _bas        = [Total Margin % Bud]
VAR _bad        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _act ) && NOT ISBLANK ( _bas ), _bas - _act )
VAR _watch      = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[watch_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "TOTAL_MARGIN" )
VAR _off        = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[off_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "TOTAL_MARGIN" )
VAR _r          = ROUND ( _bad, 10 )
RETURN
    IF (
        NOT ISBLANK ( _bad )
        ,SWITCH (
            TRUE ()
            ,_r <= _watch
                ,1
            ,_r <= _off
                ,2
            ,3
        )
    )
```

```dax
Status WEEKS_OF_COVER =

VAR _off        = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[off_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "WEEKS_OF_COVER" )

RETURN

        NOT ISBLANK ( _bad )
```


**Status Count On**

```dax
Status Count On =
VAR _t          =
    {
        [Status NET_SALES]
        ,[Status TOTAL_MARGIN]
        ,[Status WEEKS_OF_COVER]
        ,[Status MARKDOWN]
        ,[Status AVAILABILITY]
        ,[Status EBITDA]
        ,[Status LFL_GROWTH]
        ,[Status FRONT_GM]
        ,[Status SELL_THROUGH]
        ,[Status GMROI]
        ,[Status SUPPLIER_OTIF]
        ,[Status CHANNEL_MIX]
    }
RETURN
    COUNTROWS ( FILTER ( _t, [Value] = 1 ) ) + 0
```

**Status Dot**

```dax
Status Dot =
VAR _t          =
    {
        [Status NET_SALES]
        ,[Status TOTAL_MARGIN]
        ,[Status WEEKS_OF_COVER]
        ,[Status MARKDOWN]
        ,[Status AVAILABILITY]
        ,[Status EBITDA]
        ,[Status LFL_GROWTH]
        ,[Status FRONT_GM]
        ,[Status SELL_THROUGH]
        ,[Status GMROI]
        ,[Status SUPPLIER_OTIF]
        ,[Status CHANNEL_MIX]
    }
VAR _on         = COUNTROWS ( FILTER ( _t, [Value] = 1 ) ) + 0
VAR _watch      = COUNTROWS ( FILTER ( _t, [Value] = 2 ) ) + 0
VAR _offc       = COUNTROWS ( FILTER ( _t, [Value] = 3 ) ) + 0
VAR _n          = _on + _watch + _offc
VAR _red        = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[threshold], param_status_rule[rule_code], "DOT_RED_OFF" )
VAR _orange     = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[threshold], param_status_rule[rule_code], "DOT_ORANGE_WATCH_OFF" )
VAR _green      = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[threshold], param_status_rule[rule_code], "DOT_GREEN_GOOD" )
RETURN
    IF (
        _n > 0
        ,SWITCH (
            TRUE ()
            ,DIVIDE ( _offc, _n ) >= _red
                ,3
            ,DIVIDE ( _watch + _offc, _n ) > _orange
                ,2
            ,DIVIDE ( _on, _n ) >= _green
                ,1
            ,2
        )
    )
```

**Status Pack**

```dax
Status Pack =
VAR _t          =
    {
        [Status NET_SALES]
        ,[Status TOTAL_MARGIN]
        ,[Status WEEKS_OF_COVER]
        ,[Status MARKDOWN]
        ,[Status AVAILABILITY]
        ,[Status EBITDA]
        ,[Status LFL_GROWTH]
        ,[Status FRONT_GM]
        ,[Status SELL_THROUGH]
        ,[Status GMROI]
        ,[Status SUPPLIER_OTIF]
        ,[Status CHANNEL_MIX]
    }
VAR _on         = COUNTROWS ( FILTER ( _t, [Value] = 1 ) ) + 0
VAR _watch      = COUNTROWS ( FILTER ( _t, [Value] = 2 ) ) + 0
VAR _offc       = COUNTROWS ( FILTER ( _t, [Value] = 3 ) ) + 0
VAR _n          = _on + _watch + _offc
VAR _red        = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[threshold], param_status_rule[rule_code], "DOT_RED_OFF" )
VAR _orange     = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[threshold], param_status_rule[rule_code], "DOT_ORANGE_WATCH_OFF" )
VAR _green      = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[threshold], param_status_rule[rule_code], "DOT_GREEN_GOOD" )
VAR _dot        =
    SWITCH (
        TRUE ()
        ,_n = 0
            ,0
        ,DIVIDE ( _offc, _n ) >= _red
            ,3
        ,DIVIDE ( _watch + _offc, _n ) > _orange
            ,2
        ,DIVIDE ( _on, _n ) >= _green
            ,1
        ,2
    )
RETURN
    _on * 1000 + _watch * 100 + _offc * 10 + _dot
```

**Status Summary Text**

```dax
Status Summary Text =
FORMAT ( [Revealed Count], "0" ) & " of 12 metrics · " & [Filter Picks Text]
```

</details>

## 8 Cards

The card layer: the picked item (dim_item) is the window; comparators Budget / LY / Previous item; display texts as js-exec format.ts / view.ts.

| Measure | Definition | Spec | Format | Hidden |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Window Noun` | Noun of the picked type for stat labels and sub-titles: week, month, quarter or year (E-98). | E-98 | – |  |
| `Window Status Count On` | On-track count in the card window (status line). | – | 0 |  |
| `Window Status Count Watch` | Watch count in the card window. | – | 0 |  |
| `Window Status Count Off` | Off-track count in the card window. | – | 0 |  |
| `Window Status Count NA` | N/A count in the card window. | – | 0 |  |
| `Window Status Dot` | Overall dot in the card window (ET-03). | ET-03 | 0 |  |
| `Card Value NET_SALES` | NET_SALES card value: [Net Sales] in the card window. | – | – |  |
| `Card Basis NET_SALES` | NET_SALES status basis: [Net Sales Bud] in the card window. | – | – |  |
| `Card LY NET_SALES` | NET_SALES value in the LY window (ly_week_key of every window week, E-12). | E-12 | – |  |
| `Card Prev NET_SALES` | NET_SALES value in the previous-item window (previous week / month / quarter, like-length when the item is partial, E-94). | E-94 | – |  |
| `Card Compare NET_SALES` | NET_SALES comparator value: Budget / target, LY or the previous item (E-14, E-94). | E-14, E-94 | – |  |
| `Card Status NET_SALES` | NET_SALES status in the card window (always vs budget / target, E-24). | E-24 | 0 |  |
| `Card Delta NET_SALES` | NET_SALES value - comparator. | – | – |  |
| `Card Value Text NET_SALES` | NET_SALES value as displayed (E-42, js-exec format.ts), without the unit. | E-42 | – |  |
| `Card Unit Text NET_SALES` | NET_SALES unit as displayed. | – | – |  |
| `Card Basis Text NET_SALES` | NET_SALES status basis as displayed on the i pop-up target line (E-57; same format as the card value), without the unit; n/a when blank. | E-57 | – |  |
| `Card Delta Text NET_SALES` | NET_SALES delta row vs the comparator (E-14, E-42; labels as js-exec view.ts). | E-14, E-42 | – |  |
| `Card Value TOTAL_MARGIN` | TOTAL_MARGIN card value: [Total Margin %] in the card window. | – | – |  |
| `Card Basis TOTAL_MARGIN` | TOTAL_MARGIN status basis: [Total Margin % Bud] in the card window. | – | – |  |

| `Card Prev TOTAL_MARGIN` | TOTAL_MARGIN value in the previous-item window (previous week / month / quarter, like-length when the item is partial, E-94). | E-94 | – |  |
| `Card Compare TOTAL_MARGIN` | TOTAL_MARGIN comparator value: Budget / target, LY or the previous item (E-14, E-94). | E-14, E-94 | – |  |

<details><summary>DAX</summary>

**Window Noun**

```dax
Window Noun =
SWITCH ( [_Granularity Code], "P", "month", "Q", "quarter", "FY", "year", "week" )
```

**Window Status Count On**

```dax
Window Status Count On =
VAR _s          = [_Window Start]
VAR _e          = [_Window End]
RETURN
    CALCULATE ( [Status Count On], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
```

**Window Status Count Watch**

```dax
Window Status Count Watch =
VAR _s          = [_Window Start]
VAR _e          = [_Window End]
RETURN
    CALCULATE ( [Status Count Watch], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
```

**Window Status Count Off**

```dax

VAR _s          = [_Window Start]
VAR _e          = [_Window End]
RETURN
    CALCULATE ( [Status Count Off], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
```

**Window Status Count NA**

```dax

VAR _s          = [_Window Start]
VAR _e          = [_Window End]
RETURN
    CALCULATE ( [Status Count NA], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
```

**Card Value NET_SALES**

```dax
Card Value NET_SALES =

VAR _e          = [_Window End]
RETURN
    CALCULATE ( [Net Sales], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
```

**Card Basis NET_SALES**

```dax
RETURN
```

```dax
Revealed Count =

```

**Filter Picks Text**

```dax
Filter Picks Text =
VAR _group      = IF ( ISFILTERED ( param_attr_group ), CONCATENATEX ( VALUES ( param_attr_group ), param_attr_group[attr_label], ", ", param_attr_group[sort_order], ASC ) )
VAR _lever      = IF ( ISFILTERED ( param_attr_lever ), CONCATENATEX ( VALUES ( param_attr_lever ), param_attr_lever[attr_label], ", ", param_attr_lever[sort_order], ASC ) )
VAR _owner      = IF ( ISFILTERED ( param_attr_owner ), CONCATENATEX ( VALUES ( param_attr_owner ), param_attr_owner[attr_label], ", ", param_attr_owner[sort_order], ASC ) )
VAR _all        =
    CONCATENATEX (
        FILTER ( { ( 1, _group ), ( 2, _lever ), ( 3, _owner ) }, [Value2] <> "" )
        ,[Value2]
        ,", "
        ,[Value1], ASC
    )
RETURN
    IF ( _all = "", "All", _all )
```

**Filter Pick Count**

```dax
Filter Pick Count =
IF ( ISFILTERED ( param_attr_group ), COUNTROWS ( VALUES ( param_attr_group[attr_code] ) ), 0 ) + IF ( ISFILTERED ( param_attr_lever ), COUNTROWS ( VALUES ( param_attr_lever[attr_code] ) ), 0 ) + IF ( ISFILTERED ( param_attr_owner ), COUNTROWS ( VALUES ( param_attr_owner[attr_code] ) ), 0 )
```

**Header Period Text**

```dax
Header Period Text =
VAR _k          = [_Item Key]
RETURN
    CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_item[header] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item ), dim_item[item_key] = _k )
```

**Header Comparator Text**

```dax
Header Comparator Text =
SWITCH (
    [_Comparator Code]
    ,"LY"
        ,"vs **Last year** " & IF ( [_Granularity Code] = "W", "(same retail week)", "(same retail weeks)" )
    ,"LW"
        ,"vs **" & SWITCH ( [_Granularity Code], "P", "Previous month", "Q", "Previous quarter", "Previous week" ) & "**"
    ,"vs **Budget**"
)
```

**Header Region Text**

```dax
Header Region Text =
SWITCH (
    [_Scope Code]
    ,"NL"
        ,"**Netherlands** · all formats"
    ,"BE"
        ,"**Belgium** · all formats"
    ,"ONLINE"
        ,"**Online** only"
    ,"All regions & formats"
) & [_Category Label]
```

**Refreshed Text**

```dax
Refreshed Text =
VAR _r          = MAX ( meta_dataset[refreshed] )
RETURN
    "Refreshed " & FORMAT ( _r, "ddd d mmm hh:nn" )
```

**Updated Text**

```dax
Updated Text =
VAR _r          = MAX ( meta_dataset[refreshed] )
RETURN
    "Updated " & FORMAT ( _r, "ddd hh:nn" )
```

**Period Phrase**

```dax
Period Phrase =

RETURN

```

**Item Title**

```dax
Item Title =

RETURN
```

**Period No**

```dax
Period No =
VAR _e          = [_Window End]
RETURN
    CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
```

**Region Phrase**

```dax
Region Phrase =
SWITCH ( [_Scope Code], "NL", "Netherlands", "BE", "Belgium", "ONLINE", "online", "all channels" )
```

**Window Status Pack**

```dax
Window Status Pack =
VAR _s          = [_Window Start]
VAR _e          = [_Window End]
RETURN
    CALCULATE ( [Status Pack], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
```

**Card Delta Long Text EBITDA**

```dax
Card Delta Long Text EBITDA =
VAR _nm         = [_EB Prev Name]
VAR _v          = [Card Value EBITDA]
VAR _c          = [Card Compare EBITDA]
VAR _d          = _v - _c
VAR _lab        = "vs " & _nm
RETURN
    IF (
        [_Comparator Code] = "LW" && [_Granularity Code] = "W" && _nm <> "" && NOT [_Category Active]
        ,IF (
            ISBLANK ( _v ) || ISBLANK ( _c )
            ,"n/a " & _lab
            ,( VAR _f47 = _d RETURN IF ( _f47 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f47 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f47 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f47 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) & " (" & ( VAR _f48 = DIVIDE ( _v, _c ) - 1 RETURN IF ( _f48 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f48 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) & ") " & _lab
        )
        ,[Card Delta Text EBITDA]
    )
```

**Card Sub NET_SALES**

```dax
Card Sub NET_SALES =
[Period Phrase] & ", " & [Region Phrase] & IF ( LOOKUPVALUE ( dim_kpi[is_provisional], dim_kpi[kpi_code], "NET_SALES" ), " · prov.", "" )
```

**Card Sub TOTAL_MARGIN**

```dax
Card Sub TOTAL_MARGIN =
"front + back" & IF ( LOOKUPVALUE ( dim_kpi[is_provisional], dim_kpi[kpi_code], "TOTAL_MARGIN" ), " · prov.", "" )
```

**Card Sub WEEKS_OF_COVER**

```dax
Card Sub WEEKS_OF_COVER =
"forward" & IF ( LOOKUPVALUE ( dim_kpi[is_provisional], dim_kpi[kpi_code], "WEEKS_OF_COVER" ), " · prov.", "" )
```

**Card Sub MARKDOWN**

```dax
Card Sub MARKDOWN =
"% of gross sales" & IF ( LOOKUPVALUE ( dim_kpi[is_provisional], dim_kpi[kpi_code], "MARKDOWN" ), " · prov.", "" )
```

**Card Sub AVAILABILITY**

```dax
Card Sub AVAILABILITY =
"top-500 SKUs, weighted" & IF ( LOOKUPVALUE ( dim_kpi[is_provisional], dim_kpi[kpi_code], "AVAILABILITY" ), " · prov.", "" )
```

**Card Sub EBITDA**

```dax
Card Sub EBITDA =
IF ( [_Granularity Code] = "W", "month " & [Period No] & " " & "to date", [Period Phrase] ) & IF ( LOOKUPVALUE ( dim_kpi[is_provisional], dim_kpi[kpi_code], "EBITDA" ), " · prov.", "" )
```

**Card Sub LFL_GROWTH**

```dax
Card Sub LFL_GROWTH =
"vs LY" & " " & [Window Noun] & IF ( LOOKUPVALUE ( dim_kpi[is_provisional], dim_kpi[kpi_code], "LFL_GROWTH" ), " · prov.", "" )
```

**Card Sub FRONT_GM**

```dax
Card Sub FRONT_GM =
"% of net sales" & IF ( LOOKUPVALUE ( dim_kpi[is_provisional], dim_kpi[kpi_code], "FRONT_GM" ), " · prov.", "" )
```

**Card Sub SELL_THROUGH**


**Card Sub GMROI**

```dax

```


**Card Bar Target NET_SALES**

```dax
Card Bar Target NET_SALES =
VAR _s          = [_Window Start]
VAR _e          = [_Window End]
RETURN
    CALCULATE ( [Net Sales Bud], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
```

**Explain Pack**

```dax
Explain Pack =
SWITCH (
    [KPI Code]
    ,"NET_SALES"
        ,[Explain Pack NET_SALES]
    ,"TOTAL_MARGIN"
        ,[Explain Pack TOTAL_MARGIN]
    ,"WEEKS_OF_COVER"
        ,[Explain Pack WEEKS_OF_COVER]
    ,"MARKDOWN"
        ,[Explain Pack MARKDOWN]
    ,"AVAILABILITY"

    ,"EBITDA"

    ,"LFL_GROWTH"
        ,[Explain Pack LFL_GROWTH]
    ,"FRONT_GM"
        ,[Explain Pack FRONT_GM]
    ,"SELL_THROUGH"
        ,[Explain Pack SELL_THROUGH]
    ,"GMROI"
        ,[Explain Pack GMROI]
    ,"SUPPLIER_OTIF"
        ,[Explain Pack SUPPLIER_OTIF]
    ,"CHANNEL_MIX"
        ,[Explain Pack CHANNEL_MIX]
)
```

| `Rule SVG SUPPLIER_OTIF` | SUPPLIER_OTIF status-chip tooltip: status word and the rule (E-21, E-22). | E-21, E-22 | – | yes |
| `Rule SVG CHANNEL_MIX` | CHANNEL_MIX status-chip tooltip: status word and the rule (E-21, E-22). | E-21, E-22 | – | yes |
| `Header SVG` | Header (E-51): brand, period · comparator · region line, refresh stamp, bottom rule. | E-51 | – |  |
| `Status Line SVG` | Status line: overall dot (static, E-26), three count pills (E-25), n of 12 metrics · picks (E-56). | E-25, E-26, E-56 | – |  |
| `Filter Badge SVG` | Side-bar badge: active attribute picks; empty when none (E-52). | E-52 | – |  |
| `Dot SVG` | Overall-dot tooltip (tt_dot): rule and current shares over the rated cards; grey, no rated card, when none has a status (E-26, E-95; js-exec view.ts dotView). | E-26, E-95 | – |  |
| `How To Read SVG` | How to read pop-up: the six bullets of js-exec view.ts how() (E-58). | E-58 | – |  |
| `Menu Showing SVG` | Filters menu: live summary under the period picker = the header period line (E-90; js-exec menu showing). | E-90 | – |  |

## 8 Catalogue

Web sync 2026-11-16 (vosk.dev build spec, Option B): the generic catalogue over `param_metric` (82 metric codes, spec `Metric`). `[Actual]` / `[Basis]` / `[Last Year]` / `[Previous]` evaluate the one picked metric in the picked window; the EBITDA family (flash = 1) reads month to date at Week and its Previous is the previous month cut to the same weeks. The answer key (tests/web-sync.dax T-N11) reads them.

| Measure | Definition | Spec | Format | Hidden |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Metric Value` | The model measure of the one param_metric[metric_code] in context, over the dim_week rows in context (spec Metric Value). | – | – | yes |
| `Actual` | Catalogue: the metric of param_metric in the picked window; the EBITDA family (flash) reads month to date at Week (spec Actual, answer key 'actual'). | – | 0.000000 |  |
| `Last Year` | Catalogue: the metric in the LY window (same retail weeks by ly_week_key; month to date for the flash family at Week); blank in FY2025 (spec Last Year). | – | 0.000000 |  |
| `Previous` | Catalogue: the metric in the previous like-length window; the flash family at Week reads the previous month cut to the same number of weeks (spec Previous). | – | 0.000000 |  |
| `Basis` | Catalogue: the status basis (budget / target / plan) of the metric in the picked window; blank for a metric without basis (spec Basis). | – | 0.000000 |  |
| `Comparison` | Catalogue: the comparator value of the metric: Basis, Last Year or Previous (spec Comparison). | – | 0.000000 |  |

**Actual**

```dax
Actual =
VAR _flash      = SELECTEDVALUE ( param_metric[flash] ) = 1 && [_Granularity Code] = "W"
VAR _s          = [_Window Start]
VAR _e          = [_Window End]
VAR _ps         =
    IF (
        _flash
        ,VAR _p = CALCULATE ( SELECTEDVALUE ( dim_week[period_key] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
         RETURN
            CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[period_key] = _p )
        ,_s
    )
RETURN
    IF (
        NOT ISBLANK ( _s ) && NOT ISBLANK ( _e )
        ,CALCULATE (
            [Metric Value]
            ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week )
            ,dim_week[sort_index] >= _ps
            ,dim_week[sort_index] <= _e
        )
    )
```

**Last Year**

```dax
Last Year =
VAR _flash      = SELECTEDVALUE ( param_metric[flash] ) = 1 && [_Granularity Code] = "W"
VAR _s          = [_LY Window Start]
VAR _e          = [_LY Window End]
VAR _ps         =
    IF (
        _flash
        ,VAR _p = CALCULATE ( SELECTEDVALUE ( dim_week[period_key] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
         RETURN
            CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[period_key] = _p )
        ,_s
    )
RETURN
    IF (
        NOT ISBLANK ( _s ) && NOT ISBLANK ( _e )
        ,CALCULATE (
            [Metric Value]
            ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week )
            ,dim_week[sort_index] >= _ps
            ,dim_week[sort_index] <= _e
        )
    )
```

**Previous**

```dax
Previous =
VAR _flash      = SELECTEDVALUE ( param_metric[flash] ) = 1 && [_Granularity Code] = "W"
VAR _s          = IF ( _flash, [_EB Prev Start], [_Prev Window Start] )
VAR _e          = IF ( _flash, [_EB Prev End], [_Prev Window End] )
VAR _ps         = _s
RETURN
    IF (
        NOT ISBLANK ( _s ) && NOT ISBLANK ( _e )
        ,CALCULATE (
            [Metric Value]
            ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week )
            ,dim_week[sort_index] >= _ps
            ,dim_week[sort_index] <= _e
        )
    )
```

**Basis**

```dax
Basis =
VAR _basis      = SELECTEDVALUE ( param_metric[basis_code] )
RETURN
    IF (
        _basis <> ""
        ,CALCULATE (
            [Actual]
            ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_metric )
            ,param_metric[metric_code] = _basis
        )
    )
```

**Comparison**

```dax
Comparison =
SWITCH (
    [_Comparator Code]
    ,"LY"
        ,[Last Year]
    ,"LW"
        ,[Previous]
    ,[Basis]
)
```

</details>

## 8 Explain

Web sync phase 2 (2026-11-16): the Explain page texts as plain measures (spec 04 folder 7), built in one frame per card from the same DAX as Info SVG (`Explain Pack <KPI>`, fields joined with '|'); `Explain Pack` dispatches on the card picked on the Explain page (dim_kpi). Tested against the spec's ⓘ pop-up strings (tests/web-sync.dax T-N13).

| Measure | Definition | Spec | Format | Hidden |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Explain Pack NET_SALES` | NET_SALES: the Explain page texts in one frame (eyebrow, delta, 3 stats, worked example with ** around the bold run, footer note, drill label), '\|'-separated; same DAX as Info SVG (web sync phase 2). | – | – | yes |
| `Explain Pack TOTAL_MARGIN` | TOTAL_MARGIN: the Explain page texts in one frame (eyebrow, delta, 3 stats, worked example with ** around the bold run, footer note, drill label), '\|'-separated; same DAX as Info SVG (web sync phase 2). | – | – | yes |
| `Explain Pack WEEKS_OF_COVER` | WEEKS_OF_COVER: the Explain page texts in one frame (eyebrow, delta, 3 stats, worked example with ** around the bold run, footer note, drill label), '\|'-separated; same DAX as Info SVG (web sync phase 2). | – | – | yes |
| `Explain Pack MARKDOWN` | MARKDOWN: the Explain page texts in one frame (eyebrow, delta, 3 stats, worked example with ** around the bold run, footer note, drill label), '\|'-separated; same DAX as Info SVG (web sync phase 2). | – | – | yes |
| `Explain Pack AVAILABILITY` | AVAILABILITY: the Explain page texts in one frame (eyebrow, delta, 3 stats, worked example with ** around the bold run, footer note, drill label), '\|'-separated; same DAX as Info SVG (web sync phase 2). | – | – | yes |
| `Explain Pack EBITDA` | EBITDA: the Explain page texts in one frame (eyebrow, delta, 3 stats, worked example with ** around the bold run, footer note, drill label), '\|'-separated; same DAX as Info SVG (web sync phase 2). | – | – | yes |
| `Explain Pack LFL_GROWTH` | LFL_GROWTH: the Explain page texts in one frame (eyebrow, delta, 3 stats, worked example with ** around the bold run, footer note, drill label), '\|'-separated; same DAX as Info SVG (web sync phase 2). | – | – | yes |
| `Explain Pack FRONT_GM` | FRONT_GM: the Explain page texts in one frame (eyebrow, delta, 3 stats, worked example with ** around the bold run, footer note, drill label), '\|'-separated; same DAX as Info SVG (web sync phase 2). | – | – | yes |
| `Explain Pack SELL_THROUGH` | SELL_THROUGH: the Explain page texts in one frame (eyebrow, delta, 3 stats, worked example with ** around the bold run, footer note, drill label), '\|'-separated; same DAX as Info SVG (web sync phase 2). | – | – | yes |
| `Explain Pack GMROI` | GMROI: the Explain page texts in one frame (eyebrow, delta, 3 stats, worked example with ** around the bold run, footer note, drill label), '\|'-separated; same DAX as Info SVG (web sync phase 2). | – | – | yes |
| `Explain Pack SUPPLIER_OTIF` | SUPPLIER_OTIF: the Explain page texts in one frame (eyebrow, delta, 3 stats, worked example with ** around the bold run, footer note, drill label), '\|'-separated; same DAX as Info SVG (web sync phase 2). | – | – | yes |
| `Explain Pack CHANNEL_MIX` | CHANNEL_MIX: the Explain page texts in one frame (eyebrow, delta, 3 stats, worked example with ** around the bold run, footer note, drill label), '\|'-separated; same DAX as Info SVG (web sync phase 2). | – | – | yes |
| `Explain Eyebrow` | Eyebrow above the title: 'Tier 1 · ' (hero cards) or 'Explains Tier 1 · ' + the card sub-title (web explain.eyebrow). Card = dim_kpi in context (Explain page picker). | – | – |  |
| `Explain Delta` | Delta line of the explanation; EBITDA at Week vs Previous names the compared weeks (vs P9 weeks 1–4). Card = dim_kpi in context (Explain page picker). | – | – |  |
| `Explain Stat 1 Label` | Mini stat 1: label. Card = dim_kpi in context (Explain page picker). | – | – |  |
| `Explain Stat 1 Value` | Mini stat 1: value (n/a under a category pick the card cannot split). Card = dim_kpi in context (Explain page picker). | – | – |  |
