| `Explain Stat 3 Value` | Mini stat 3: value. Card = dim_kpi in context (Explain page picker). | – | – |  |
| `Explain Updated` | Footer note closing the tags row: provisional note, 'Updated weekly' or 'Updated <refresh>'. Card = dim_kpi in context (Explain page picker). | – | – |  |
| `Explain Drill Label` | Label of the drill pill: 'Drill path · <drill page>'. Card = dim_kpi in context (Explain page picker). | – | – |  |
| `Example Pre` | Worked example, text before the bold run (the whole sentence when it has none). | – | – |  |
| `Example Bold` | Worked example, the bold run (no-break spaces of the SVG line wrap turned back into spaces). | – | – |  |
| `Example Post` | Worked example, text after the bold run. | – | – |  |

<details><summary>DAX</summary>

**Explain Pack NET_SALES**

```dax
Explain Pack NET_SALES =
VAR _k          = [_Item Key]
VAR _it         = CALCULATETABLE ( dim_item, REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item ), dim_item[item_key] = _k )
VAR _s          = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[start_index] )
VAR _e          = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[end_index] )
VAR _ls         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[ly_start] )
VAR _le         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[ly_end] )
VAR _s2         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[prev_start] )
VAR _e2         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[prev_end] )
VAR _iso        = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[sort_order] )
VAR _phrase     = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[phrase] )
VAR _gr         = [_Granularity Code]
VAR _cmp        = [_Comparator Code]
VAR _sc         = [_Scope Code]
VAR _pshort     = [_Prev Short]
VAR _noun       = [Window Noun]
VAR _rph        = [Region Phrase]
VAR _rev        = [Card Revealed NET_SALES]
VAR _pno        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _cact       = [_Category Active]
VAR _cna        = FALSE ()
VAR _wpk        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period_key] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wq         = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[quarter] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wfy        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[fy] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wps        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[period_key] = _wpk )
VAR _wqs        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy, dim_week[quarter] = _wq )
VAR _wys        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy )
VAR _ps         = IF ( _gr = "W", _wps, _s )
RETURN
    CALCULATE (
        VAR _h1        = CALCULATE ( [Net Sales], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h2        = CALCULATE ( [Net Sales Bud], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h3        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _ls ), CALCULATE ( [Net Sales], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ls, dim_week[sort_index] <= _le ) )
        VAR _h4        = CALCULATE ( [Gross Sales], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h5        = CALCULATE ( [Markdown], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h6        = CALCULATE ( [Returns], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h7        = CALCULATE ( [Net Sales], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _wps, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h8        = CALCULATE ( [Net Sales], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _wqs, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h9        = CALCULATE ( [Net Sales], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _wys, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h10       = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _s2 ), CALCULATE ( [Net Sales], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s2, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e2 ) )
        VAR _v          = _h1
        VAR _b          = _h2
        VAR _c          = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", _h3, "LW", _h10, _b )
        VAR _d          = _v - _c
        VAR _lab        = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", "vs LY", "LW", _pshort, "vs budget" )
        VAR _dt         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ) || ISBLANK ( _c ), "n/a " & _lab, ( VAR _f900003 = _d RETURN IF ( _f900003 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f900003 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900003 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900003 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) & " (" & ( VAR _f900004 = DIVIDE ( _v, _c ) - 1 RETURN IF ( _f900004 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900004 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) & ") " & _lab )
        VAR _bad        = IF ( _b <> 0, 1 - DIVIDE ( _v, _b ) )
        VAR _sw         = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[watch_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "NET_SALES" )
        VAR _so         = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[off_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "NET_SALES" )
        VAR _rb         = ROUND ( _bad, 10 )
        VAR _st         = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _bad ), SWITCH ( TRUE (), _rb <= _sw, 1, _rb <= _so, 2, 3 ) )
        VAR _val        = IF ( _cna, "–", IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ), "n/a", ( VAR _f900001 = _v RETURN IF ( _f900001 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f900001 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900001 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900001 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) ) ) )
        VAR _unit       = IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ), "", "m" )
        VAR _bt         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _b ), "n/a", ( VAR _f900002 = _b RETURN IF ( _f900002 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f900002 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900002 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900002 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) ) )
        VAR _dlong      = _dt
        VAR _sub        = _phrase & ", " & _rph & IF ( LOOKUPVALUE ( dim_kpi[is_provisional], dim_kpi[kpi_code], "NET_SALES" ), " · prov.", "" )
        VAR _ink        = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#136C3A", 2, "#9A5B00", 3, "#C0272D", "#5F6878" )
        VAR _cur        = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#136C3A", 2, "#9A5B00", 3, "#C0272D", "#111827" )
        VAR _tint       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#E1F0E5", 2, "#FBEFD6", 3, "#FAE3E3", "#F1F3F5" )
        VAR _chip       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "▲ On track", 2, "● Watch", 3, "▼ Off track", "– N/A" )
        VAR _glyph      = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "▲", 2, "●", 3, "▼", "–" )
        VAR _word       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "On track", 2, "Watch", 3, "Off track", "N/A" )
        VAR _cw         = SWITCH ( _st, 1, 74.8, 2, 63.5, 3, 76.0, 51.2 )
        VAR _sl0        = "vs LY " & _noun
        VAR _sv0        = IF ( _cna && _sl0 <> "", "n/a", IF ( _h3 = 0, "n/a", ( VAR _f198 = DIVIDE ( _h1, _h3 ) - 1 RETURN IF ( _f198 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f198 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) ) )
        VAR _sl1        = SWITCH ( _gr, "W", "Month " & _pno & " to date", "P", "Quarter " & _wq & " to date", "Q", "FY to date", "Budget full year" )
        VAR _sv1        = IF ( _cna && _sl1 <> "", "n/a", SWITCH ( _gr, "W", ( VAR _f191 = ( _h7 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f191 ), "n/a", IF ( _f191 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f191 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f191 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f191 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ), "P", ( VAR _f192 = ( _h8 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f192 ), "n/a", IF ( _f192 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f192 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f192 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f192 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ), "Q", ( VAR _f193 = ( _h9 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f193 ), "n/a", IF ( _f193 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f193 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f193 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f193 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ), ( VAR _f194 = CALCULATE ( [Net Sales Bud], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f194 ), "n/a", IF ( _f194 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f194 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f194 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f194 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) ) )
        VAR _sl2        = SWITCH ( _gr, "Q", "Previous quarter", "FY", "Last year", "Year to date" )
        VAR _sv2        = IF ( _cna && _sl2 <> "", "n/a", SWITCH ( _gr, "Q", ( VAR _f195 = _h10 RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f195 ), "n/a", IF ( _f195 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f195 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f195 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f195 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ), "FY", ( VAR _f196 = _h3 RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f196 ), "n/a", IF ( _f196 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f196 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f196 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f196 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ), ( VAR _f197 = ( _h9 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f197 ), "n/a", IF ( _f197 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f197 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f197 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f197 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) ) )
        VAR _ex         = ( VAR _f184 = ( _h4 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f184 ), "n/a", IF ( _f184 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f184 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f184 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f184 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & " at ticket − " & ( VAR _f185 = ( _h5 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f185 ), "n/a", IF ( _f185 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f185 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f185 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f185 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & " markdowns and discounts − " & ( VAR _f186 = ( _h6 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f186 ), "n/a", IF ( _f186 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f186 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f186 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f186 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & " returns = " & "**" & SUBSTITUTE ( ( VAR _f187 = ( _h1 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f187 ), "n/a", IF ( _f187 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f187 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f187 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f187 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ), " ", UNICHAR ( 160 ) ) & "**" & IF ( ISBLANK ( _h2 ), ".", ". Budget was " & ( VAR _f188 = _h2 RETURN IF ( _f188 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f188 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f188 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f188 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) & ", so " & ( VAR _f189 = _h1 - _h2 RETURN IF ( _f189 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f189 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f189 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f189 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) & " (" & ( VAR _f190 = DIVIDE ( _h1, _h2 ) - 1 RETURN IF ( _f190 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f190 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) & ")." )
        VAR _footr      = [Updated Text]
        VAR _pk         =
            SUBSTITUTE ( "Tier 1 · " & _sub, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _dlong, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl0, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv0, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl1, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv1, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl2, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv2, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _ex, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _footr, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( "Drill path · Sales page", "|", "/" )
        RETURN
            _pk
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_region )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_granularity )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_comparator )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_group )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_lever )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_owner )
        ,FILTER ( ALL ( dim_store[scope_code] ), _sc = "ALL" || dim_store[scope_code] = _sc )
    )
```

**Explain Pack TOTAL_MARGIN**

```dax
Explain Pack TOTAL_MARGIN =
VAR _k          = [_Item Key]
VAR _it         = CALCULATETABLE ( dim_item, REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item ), dim_item[item_key] = _k )
VAR _s          = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[start_index] )
VAR _e          = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[end_index] )
VAR _ls         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[ly_start] )
VAR _le         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[ly_end] )
VAR _s2         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[prev_start] )
VAR _e2         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[prev_end] )
VAR _iso        = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[sort_order] )
VAR _phrase     = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[phrase] )
VAR _gr         = [_Granularity Code]
VAR _cmp        = [_Comparator Code]
VAR _sc         = [_Scope Code]
VAR _pshort     = [_Prev Short]
VAR _noun       = [Window Noun]
VAR _rph        = [Region Phrase]
VAR _rev        = [Card Revealed TOTAL_MARGIN]
VAR _pno        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _cact       = [_Category Active]
VAR _cna        = FALSE ()
VAR _wpk        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period_key] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wq         = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[quarter] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wfy        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[fy] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wps        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[period_key] = _wpk )
VAR _wqs        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy, dim_week[quarter] = _wq )
VAR _wys        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy )
VAR _ps         = IF ( _gr = "W", _wps, _s )
RETURN
    CALCULATE (
        VAR _h1        = CALCULATE ( [Total Margin %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h2        = CALCULATE ( [Total Margin % Bud], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h3        = CALCULATE ( [Front Margin], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h4        = CALCULATE ( [Back Margin], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h5        = CALCULATE ( [Total Margin], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h6        = CALCULATE ( [Net Sales], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h7        = CALCULATE ( [Front GM %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h8        = CALCULATE ( [Back Margin %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h9        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _ls ), CALCULATE ( [Total Margin %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ls, dim_week[sort_index] <= _le ) )
        VAR _h10       = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _s2 ), CALCULATE ( [Total Margin %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s2, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e2 ) )
        VAR _v          = _h1
        VAR _b          = _h2
        VAR _c          = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", _h9, "LW", _h10, _b )
        VAR _d          = _v - _c
        VAR _lab        = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", "vs LY", "LW", _pshort, "vs budget" )
        VAR _dt         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ) || ISBLANK ( _c ), "n/a " & _lab, ( VAR _f900004 = _d RETURN IF ( _f900004 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900004 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) & " " & _lab & " " & IF ( _cmp = "BUD", ( VAR _f900003 = _c RETURN IF ( _f900003 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900003 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ), ( VAR _f900005 = _c RETURN IF ( _f900005 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900005 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) ) )
        VAR _bad        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _v ) && NOT ISBLANK ( _b ), _b - _v )
        VAR _sw         = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[watch_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "TOTAL_MARGIN" )
        VAR _so         = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[off_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "TOTAL_MARGIN" )
        VAR _rb         = ROUND ( _bad, 10 )
        VAR _st         = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _bad ), SWITCH ( TRUE (), _rb <= _sw, 1, _rb <= _so, 2, 3 ) )
        VAR _val        = IF ( _cna, "–", IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ), "n/a", ( VAR _f900001 = _v RETURN IF ( _f900001 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900001 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) ) ) )
        VAR _unit       = IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ), "", "%" )
        VAR _bt         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _b ), "n/a", ( VAR _f900002 = _b RETURN IF ( _f900002 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900002 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) ) )
        VAR _dlong      = _dt
        VAR _sub        = "front + back" & IF ( LOOKUPVALUE ( dim_kpi[is_provisional], dim_kpi[kpi_code], "TOTAL_MARGIN" ), " · prov.", "" )
        VAR _ink        = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#136C3A", 2, "#9A5B00", 3, "#C0272D", "#5F6878" )
        VAR _cur        = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#136C3A", 2, "#9A5B00", 3, "#C0272D", "#111827" )
        VAR _tint       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#E1F0E5", 2, "#FBEFD6", 3, "#FAE3E3", "#F1F3F5" )
        VAR _chip       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "▲ On track", 2, "● Watch", 3, "▼ Off track", "– N/A" )
        VAR _glyph      = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "▲", 2, "●", 3, "▼", "–" )
        VAR _word       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "On track", 2, "Watch", 3, "Off track", "N/A" )
        VAR _cw         = SWITCH ( _st, 1, 74.8, 2, 63.5, 3, 76.0, 51.2 )
        VAR _sl0        = "Front margin"
        VAR _sv0        = IF ( _cna && _sl0 <> "", "n/a", ( VAR _f206 = _h7 RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f206 ), "n/a", IF ( _f206 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f206 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) ) )
        VAR _sl1        = "Back margin"
        VAR _sv1        = IF ( _cna && _sl1 <> "", "n/a", ( VAR _f207 = _h8 RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f207 ), "n/a", IF ( _f207 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f207 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) ) )
        VAR _sl2        = "vs LY " & _noun
        VAR _sv2        = IF ( _cna && _sl2 <> "", "n/a", IF ( ISBLANK ( _h1 ) || ISBLANK ( _h9 ), "n/a", ( VAR _f208 = _h1 - _h9 RETURN IF ( _f208 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f208 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) ) )
        VAR _ex         = "Front " & ( VAR _f199 = ( _h3 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f199 ), "n/a", IF ( _f199 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f199 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f199 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f199 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & " + back " & ( VAR _f200 = ( _h4 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f200 ), "n/a", IF ( _f200 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f200 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f200 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f200 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & " = " & ( VAR _f201 = ( _h5 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f201 ), "n/a", IF ( _f201 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f201 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f201 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f201 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & "; ÷ " & ( VAR _f202 = ( _h6 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f202 ), "n/a", IF ( _f202 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f202 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f202 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f202 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & " = " & "**" & SUBSTITUTE ( ( VAR _f203 = _h1 RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f203 ), "n/a", IF ( _f203 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f203 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) ), " ", UNICHAR ( 160 ) ) & "**" & IF ( ISBLANK ( _h1 ) || ISBLANK ( _h2 ), ".", ". Budget " & ( VAR _f204 = _h2 RETURN IF ( _f204 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f204 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) & ", so " & ( VAR _f205 = _h1 - _h2 RETURN IF ( _f205 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f205 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) & "." )
        VAR _footr      = IF ( LOOKUPVALUE ( dim_kpi[is_provisional], dim_kpi[kpi_code], "TOTAL_MARGIN" ), "Provisional · true-up 10 Nov", [Updated Text] )
        VAR _pk         =
            SUBSTITUTE ( "Tier 1 · " & _sub, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _dlong, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl0, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv0, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl1, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv1, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl2, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv2, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _ex, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _footr, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( "Drill path · Margin page", "|", "/" )
        RETURN
            _pk
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_region )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_granularity )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_comparator )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_group )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_lever )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_owner )
        ,FILTER ( ALL ( dim_store[scope_code] ), _sc = "ALL" || dim_store[scope_code] = _sc )
    )
```

**Explain Pack WEEKS_OF_COVER**

```dax
Explain Pack WEEKS_OF_COVER =
VAR _k          = [_Item Key]
VAR _it         = CALCULATETABLE ( dim_item, REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item ), dim_item[item_key] = _k )
VAR _s          = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[start_index] )
VAR _e          = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[end_index] )
VAR _ls         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[ly_start] )
VAR _le         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[ly_end] )
VAR _s2         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[prev_start] )
VAR _e2         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[prev_end] )
VAR _iso        = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[sort_order] )
VAR _phrase     = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[phrase] )
VAR _gr         = [_Granularity Code]
VAR _cmp        = [_Comparator Code]
VAR _sc         = [_Scope Code]
VAR _pshort     = [_Prev Short]
VAR _noun       = [Window Noun]
VAR _rph        = [Region Phrase]
VAR _rev        = [Card Revealed WEEKS_OF_COVER]
VAR _pno        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _cact       = [_Category Active]
VAR _cna        = FALSE ()
VAR _wpk        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period_key] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wq         = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[quarter] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wfy        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[fy] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wps        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[period_key] = _wpk )
VAR _wqs        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy, dim_week[quarter] = _wq )
VAR _wys        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy )
VAR _ps         = IF ( _gr = "W", _wps, _s )
RETURN
    CALCULATE (
        VAR _h1        = CALCULATE ( [Weeks of Cover], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h2        = CALCULATE ( [Cover Target Wks], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h3        = CALCULATE ( [Stock incl On Order], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h4        = CALCULATE ( [Forecast COGS], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h5        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _s2 ), CALCULATE ( [Weeks of Cover], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s2, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e2 ) )
        VAR _h6        = CALCULATE ( [On Hand Cost], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h7        = CALCULATE ( [On Order Cost], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h8        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _ls ), CALCULATE ( [Weeks of Cover], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ls, dim_week[sort_index] <= _le ) )
        VAR _v          = _h1
        VAR _b          = _h2
        VAR _c          = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", _h8, "LW", _h5, _b )
        VAR _d          = _v - _c
        VAR _lab        = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", "vs LY", "LW", _pshort, "vs target" )
        VAR _dt         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ) || ISBLANK ( _c ), "n/a " & _lab, ( VAR _f900003 = _d RETURN IF ( _f900003 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900003 ), "#,0.0" ) & " wks" ) & " " & _lab & " " & ( VAR _f900004 = _c RETURN IF ( _f900004 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900004 ), "#,0.0" ) ) )
        VAR _bad        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _v ), ABS ( _v - _b ) )
        VAR _sw         = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[watch_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "WEEKS_OF_COVER" )
        VAR _so         = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[off_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "WEEKS_OF_COVER" )
        VAR _rb         = ROUND ( _bad, 10 )
        VAR _st         = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _bad ), SWITCH ( TRUE (), _rb <= _sw, 1, _rb <= _so, 2, 3 ) )
        VAR _val        = IF ( _cna, "–", IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ), "n/a", ( VAR _f900001 = _v RETURN IF ( _f900001 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900001 ), "#,0.0" ) ) ) )
        VAR _unit       = IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ), "", "wks" )
        VAR _bt         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _b ), "n/a", ( VAR _f900002 = _b RETURN IF ( _f900002 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900002 ), "#,0.0" ) ) )
        VAR _dlong      = _dt
        VAR _sub        = "forward" & IF ( LOOKUPVALUE ( dim_kpi[is_provisional], dim_kpi[kpi_code], "WEEKS_OF_COVER" ), " · prov.", "" )
        VAR _ink        = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#136C3A", 2, "#9A5B00", 3, "#C0272D", "#5F6878" )
        VAR _cur        = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#136C3A", 2, "#9A5B00", 3, "#C0272D", "#111827" )
        VAR _tint       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#E1F0E5", 2, "#FBEFD6", 3, "#FAE3E3", "#F1F3F5" )
        VAR _chip       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "▲ On track", 2, "● Watch", 3, "▼ Off track", "– N/A" )
        VAR _glyph      = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "▲", 2, "●", 3, "▼", "–" )
        VAR _word       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "On track", 2, "Watch", 3, "Off track", "N/A" )
        VAR _cw         = SWITCH ( _st, 1, 74.8, 2, 63.5, 3, 76.0, 51.2 )
        VAR _sl0        = "On hand at cost"
        VAR _sv0        = IF ( _cna && _sl0 <> "", "n/a", ( VAR _f214 = ( _h6 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f214 ), "n/a", IF ( _f214 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f214 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ) & "m" ) ) )
        VAR _sl1        = "On order"
        VAR _sv1        = IF ( _cna && _sl1 <> "", "n/a", ( VAR _f215 = ( _h7 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f215 ), "n/a", IF ( _f215 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f215 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ) & "m" ) ) )
        VAR _sl2        = IF ( _gr = "W", "vs last week", _pshort )
        VAR _sv2        = IF ( _cna && _sl2 <> "", "n/a", IF ( ISBLANK ( _h1 ) || ISBLANK ( _h5 ), "n/a", ( VAR _f216 = _h1 - _h5 RETURN IF ( _f216 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f216 ), "#,0.0" ) & " wks" ) ) )
        VAR _ex         = ( VAR _f209 = ( _h3 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f209 ), "n/a", IF ( _f209 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f209 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ) & "m" ) ) & " stock incl. on-order ÷ " & ( VAR _f210 = ( _h4 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f210 ), "n/a", IF ( _f210 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f210 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f210 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f210 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & " forecast weekly cost of goods = " & "**" & SUBSTITUTE ( IF ( ISBLANK ( _h1 ), "n/a", ( VAR _f211 = _h1 RETURN IF ( _f211 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f211 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & " weeks" ), " ", UNICHAR ( 160 ) ) & "**" & IF ( ISBLANK ( _h1 ) || ISBLANK ( _h2 ), ".", ". Target " & ( VAR _f212 = _h2 RETURN IF ( _f212 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f212 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & ", so " & ( VAR _f213 = _h1 - _h2 RETURN IF ( _f213 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f213 ), "#,0.0" ) & "" ) & " → " & _word & "." )
        VAR _footr      = [Updated Text]
        VAR _pk         =
            SUBSTITUTE ( "Tier 1 · " & _sub, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _dlong, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl0, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv0, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl1, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv1, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl2, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv2, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _ex, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _footr, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( "Drill path · Inventory page", "|", "/" )
        RETURN
            _pk
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_region )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_granularity )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_comparator )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_group )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_lever )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_owner )
        ,FILTER ( ALL ( dim_store[scope_code] ), _sc = "ALL" || dim_store[scope_code] = _sc )
    )
```

**Explain Pack MARKDOWN**

```dax
Explain Pack MARKDOWN =
VAR _k          = [_Item Key]
VAR _it         = CALCULATETABLE ( dim_item, REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item ), dim_item[item_key] = _k )
VAR _s          = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[start_index] )
VAR _e          = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[end_index] )
VAR _ls         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[ly_start] )
VAR _le         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[ly_end] )
VAR _s2         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[prev_start] )
VAR _e2         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[prev_end] )
VAR _iso        = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[sort_order] )
VAR _phrase     = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[phrase] )
VAR _gr         = [_Granularity Code]
VAR _cmp        = [_Comparator Code]
VAR _sc         = [_Scope Code]
VAR _pshort     = [_Prev Short]
VAR _noun       = [Window Noun]
VAR _rph        = [Region Phrase]
VAR _rev        = [Card Revealed MARKDOWN]
VAR _pno        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _cact       = [_Category Active]
VAR _cna        = FALSE ()
VAR _wpk        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period_key] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wq         = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[quarter] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wfy        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[fy] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wps        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[period_key] = _wpk )
VAR _wqs        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy, dim_week[quarter] = _wq )
VAR _wys        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy )
VAR _ps         = IF ( _gr = "W", _wps, _s )
RETURN
    CALCULATE (
        VAR _h1        = CALCULATE ( [Markdown %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h2        = CALCULATE ( [Markdown % Bud], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h3        = CALCULATE ( [Markdown], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h4        = CALCULATE ( [Gross Sales], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h5        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _ls ), CALCULATE ( [Markdown %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ls, dim_week[sort_index] <= _le ) )
        VAR _h6        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _s2 ), CALCULATE ( [Markdown %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s2, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e2 ) )
        VAR _v          = _h1
        VAR _b          = _h2
        VAR _c          = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", _h5, "LW", _h6, _b )
        VAR _d          = _v - _c
        VAR _lab        = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", "vs LY", "LW", _pshort, "vs budget" )
        VAR _dt         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ) || ISBLANK ( _c ), "n/a " & _lab, ( VAR _f900004 = _d RETURN IF ( _f900004 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900004 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) & " " & _lab & " " & IF ( _cmp = "BUD", ( VAR _f900003 = _c RETURN IF ( _f900003 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900003 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ), ( VAR _f900005 = _c RETURN IF ( _f900005 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900005 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) ) )
        VAR _bad        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _v ) && NOT ISBLANK ( _b ), _v - _b )
        VAR _sw         = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[watch_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "MARKDOWN" )
        VAR _so         = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[off_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "MARKDOWN" )
        VAR _rb         = ROUND ( _bad, 10 )
        VAR _st         = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _bad ), SWITCH ( TRUE (), _rb <= _sw, 1, _rb <= _so, 2, 3 ) )
        VAR _val        = IF ( _cna, "–", IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ), "n/a", ( VAR _f900001 = _v RETURN IF ( _f900001 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900001 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) ) ) )
        VAR _unit       = IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ), "", "%" )
        VAR _bt         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _b ), "n/a", ( VAR _f900002 = _b RETURN IF ( _f900002 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900002 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) ) )
        VAR _dlong      = _dt
        VAR _sub        = "% of gross sales" & IF ( LOOKUPVALUE ( dim_kpi[is_provisional], dim_kpi[kpi_code], "MARKDOWN" ), " · prov.", "" )
        VAR _ink        = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#136C3A", 2, "#9A5B00", 3, "#C0272D", "#5F6878" )
        VAR _cur        = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#136C3A", 2, "#9A5B00", 3, "#C0272D", "#111827" )
        VAR _tint       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#E1F0E5", 2, "#FBEFD6", 3, "#FAE3E3", "#F1F3F5" )
        VAR _chip       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "▲ On track", 2, "● Watch", 3, "▼ Off track", "– N/A" )
        VAR _glyph      = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "▲", 2, "●", 3, "▼", "–" )
        VAR _word       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "On track", 2, "Watch", 3, "Off track", "N/A" )
        VAR _cw         = SWITCH ( _st, 1, 74.8, 2, 63.5, 3, 76.0, 51.2 )
        VAR _sl0        = "Markdown value"
        VAR _sv0        = IF ( _cna && _sl0 <> "", "n/a", ( VAR _f222 = ( _h3 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f222 ), "n/a", IF ( _f222 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f222 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f222 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f222 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) )
        VAR _sl1        = "vs LY " & _noun
        VAR _sv1        = IF ( _cna && _sl1 <> "", "n/a", IF ( ISBLANK ( _h1 ) || ISBLANK ( _h5 ), "n/a", ( VAR _f223 = _h1 - _h5 RETURN IF ( _f223 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f223 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) ) )
        VAR _sl2        = "Budget"
        VAR _sv2        = IF ( _cna && _sl2 <> "", "n/a", ( VAR _f224 = _h2 RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f224 ), "n/a", IF ( _f224 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f224 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) ) )
        VAR _ex         = ( VAR _f217 = ( _h3 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f217 ), "n/a", IF ( _f217 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f217 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f217 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f217 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & " markdown ÷ " & ( VAR _f218 = ( _h4 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f218 ), "n/a", IF ( _f218 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f218 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f218 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f218 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & " gross sales = " & "**" & SUBSTITUTE ( ( VAR _f219 = _h1 RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f219 ), "n/a", IF ( _f219 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f219 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) ), " ", UNICHAR ( 160 ) ) & "**" & IF ( ISBLANK ( _h1 ) || ISBLANK ( _h2 ), ".", ". Budget " & ( VAR _f220 = _h2 RETURN IF ( _f220 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f220 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) & ", so " & ( VAR _f221 = _h1 - _h2 RETURN IF ( _f221 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f221 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) & "; lower is better → " & _word & "." )
        VAR _footr      = [Updated Text]
        VAR _pk         =
            SUBSTITUTE ( "Explains Tier 1 · " & _sub, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _dlong, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl0, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv0, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl1, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv1, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl2, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv2, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _ex, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _footr, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( "Drill path · Margin page", "|", "/" )
        RETURN
            _pk
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_region )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_granularity )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_comparator )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_group )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_lever )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_owner )
        ,FILTER ( ALL ( dim_store[scope_code] ), _sc = "ALL" || dim_store[scope_code] = _sc )
    )
```

**Explain Pack AVAILABILITY**

```dax
Explain Pack AVAILABILITY =
VAR _k          = [_Item Key]
VAR _it         = CALCULATETABLE ( dim_item, REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item ), dim_item[item_key] = _k )
VAR _s          = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[start_index] )
VAR _e          = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[end_index] )
VAR _ls         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[ly_start] )
VAR _le         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[ly_end] )
VAR _s2         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[prev_start] )
VAR _e2         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[prev_end] )
VAR _iso        = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[sort_order] )
VAR _phrase     = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[phrase] )
VAR _gr         = [_Granularity Code]
VAR _cmp        = [_Comparator Code]
VAR _sc         = [_Scope Code]
VAR _pshort     = [_Prev Short]
VAR _noun       = [Window Noun]
VAR _rph        = [Region Phrase]
VAR _rev        = [Card Revealed AVAILABILITY]
VAR _pno        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _cact       = [_Category Active]
VAR _cna        = FALSE ()
VAR _wpk        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period_key] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wq         = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[quarter] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wfy        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[fy] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wps        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[period_key] = _wpk )
VAR _wqs        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy, dim_week[quarter] = _wq )
VAR _wys        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy )
VAR _ps         = IF ( _gr = "W", _wps, _s )
RETURN
    CALCULATE (
        VAR _h1        = CALCULATE ( [Availability %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h2        = CALCULATE ( [Availability Target], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h3        = CALCULATE ( [Trading Store Count], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h4        = CALCULATE ( [Ranged SKU Days], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h5        = CALCULATE ( [In Stock SKU Days], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h6        = CALCULATE ( CALCULATE ( [Availability %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e ), TREATAS ( { "NL" }, param_region[scope_code] ) )
        VAR _h7        = CALCULATE ( CALCULATE ( [Availability %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e ), TREATAS ( { "BE" }, param_region[scope_code] ) )
        VAR _h8        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _s2 ), CALCULATE ( [Availability %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s2, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e2 ) )
        VAR _h9        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _ls ), CALCULATE ( [Availability %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ls, dim_week[sort_index] <= _le ) )
        VAR _v          = _h1
        VAR _b          = _h2
        VAR _c          = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", _h9, "LW", _h8, _b )
        VAR _d          = _v - _c
        VAR _lab        = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", "vs LY", "LW", _pshort, "vs target" )
        VAR _dt         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ) || ISBLANK ( _c ), "n/a " & _lab, ( VAR _f900005 = _d RETURN IF ( _f900005 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900005 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) & " " & _lab & " " & IF ( _cmp = "BUD", ( VAR _f900003 = _c RETURN ( VAR _f900004 = FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900003 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) RETURN IF ( RIGHT ( _f900004, 2 ) = ".0", LEFT ( _f900004, LEN ( _f900004 ) - 2 ), _f900004 ) ) & "%" ), ( VAR _f900006 = _c RETURN IF ( _f900006 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900006 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) ) )
        VAR _bad        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _v ), _b - _v )
        VAR _sw         = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[watch_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "AVAILABILITY" )
        VAR _so         = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[off_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "AVAILABILITY" )
        VAR _rb         = ROUND ( _bad, 10 )
        VAR _st         = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _bad ), SWITCH ( TRUE (), _rb <= _sw, 1, _rb <= _so, 2, 3 ) )
        VAR _val        = IF ( _cna, "–", IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ), "n/a", ( VAR _f900001 = _v RETURN IF ( _f900001 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900001 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) ) ) )
        VAR _unit       = IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ), "", "%" )
        VAR _bt         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _b ), "n/a", ( VAR _f900002 = _b RETURN IF ( _f900002 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900002 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) ) )
        VAR _dlong      = _dt
        VAR _sub        = "top-500 SKUs, weighted" & IF ( LOOKUPVALUE ( dim_kpi[is_provisional], dim_kpi[kpi_code], "AVAILABILITY" ), " · prov.", "" )
        VAR _ink        = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#136C3A", 2, "#9A5B00", 3, "#C0272D", "#5F6878" )
        VAR _cur        = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#136C3A", 2, "#9A5B00", 3, "#C0272D", "#111827" )
        VAR _tint       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#E1F0E5", 2, "#FBEFD6", 3, "#FAE3E3", "#F1F3F5" )
        VAR _chip       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "▲ On track", 2, "● Watch", 3, "▼ Off track", "– N/A" )
        VAR _glyph      = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "▲", 2, "●", 3, "▼", "–" )
        VAR _word       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "On track", 2, "Watch", 3, "Off track", "N/A" )
        VAR _cw         = SWITCH ( _st, 1, 74.8, 2, 63.5, 3, 76.0, 51.2 )
        VAR _sl0        = IF ( _gr = "W", "vs last week", _pshort )
        VAR _sv0        = IF ( _cna && _sl0 <> "", "n/a", IF ( ISBLANK ( _h1 ), "n/a", IF ( ISBLANK ( _h1 ) || ISBLANK ( _h8 ), "n/a", ( VAR _f233 = _h1 - _h8 RETURN IF ( _f233 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f233 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) ) ) )
        VAR _sl1        = "Netherlands · Belgium"
        VAR _sv1        = IF ( _cna && _sl1 <> "", "n/a", ( VAR _f234 = _h6 RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f234 ), "n/a", IF ( _f234 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f234 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) ) & " · " & ( VAR _f235 = _h7 RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f235 ), "n/a", IF ( _f235 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f235 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) ) )
        VAR _sl2        = "Lowest category"
        VAR _sv2        = IF ( _cna && _sl2 <> "", "n/a", ( VAR _low = TOPN ( 1, FILTER ( ADDCOLUMNS ( VALUES ( dim_category[category_name] ), "@av", CALCULATE ( [Availability %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e ) ), NOT ISBLANK ( [@av] ) ), [@av], ASC ) RETURN IF ( ISEMPTY ( _low ), "n/a", MAXX ( _low, dim_category[category_name] ) & " " & ( VAR _f232 = MAXX ( _low, [@av] ) RETURN IF ( _f232 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f232 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) ) ) )
        VAR _ex         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _h1 ), "Availability counts stores only; this selection has none.", IF ( _gr = "W", "", "Last week of the " & _noun & ": " ) & "500 SKUs × " & ( VAR _f225 = ( _h3 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f225 ), "n/a", IF ( _f225 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f225 ), "#,0" ) ) ) & " stores × 7 days = " & ( VAR _f226 = ( _h4 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f226 ), "n/a", IF ( _f226 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f226 ), "#,0" ) ) ) & " ranged; " & ( VAR _f227 = ( _h5 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f227 ), "n/a", IF ( _f227 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f227 ), "#,0" ) ) ) & " in stock → " & "**" & SUBSTITUTE ( ( VAR _f228 = _h1 RETURN IF ( _f228 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f228 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ), " ", UNICHAR ( 160 ) ) & "**" & ". Target " & ( VAR _f229 = ( _h2 + 0 ) RETURN ( VAR _f230 = FORMAT ( ABS ( _f229 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) RETURN IF ( RIGHT ( _f230, 2 ) = ".0", LEFT ( _f230, LEN ( _f230 ) - 2 ), _f230 ) ) & "%" ) & ", so " & ( VAR _f231 = _h1 - ( _h2 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( _f231 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f231 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) & " → " & _word & "." )
        VAR _footr      = [Updated Text]
        VAR _pk         =
            SUBSTITUTE ( "Explains Tier 1 · " & _sub, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _dlong, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl0, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv0, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl1, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv1, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl2, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv2, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _ex, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _footr, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( "Drill path · Inventory page", "|", "/" )
        RETURN
            _pk
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_region )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_granularity )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_comparator )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_group )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_lever )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_owner )
        ,FILTER ( ALL ( dim_store[scope_code] ), _sc = "ALL" || dim_store[scope_code] = _sc )
    )
```

**Explain Pack EBITDA**

```dax
Explain Pack EBITDA =
VAR _k          = [_Item Key]
VAR _it         = CALCULATETABLE ( dim_item, REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item ), dim_item[item_key] = _k )
VAR _s          = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[start_index] )
VAR _e          = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[end_index] )
VAR _ls         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[ly_start] )
VAR _le         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[ly_end] )
VAR _s2         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[eb_prev_start] )
VAR _e2         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[eb_prev_end] )
VAR _iso        = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[sort_order] )
VAR _phrase     = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[phrase] )
VAR _gr         = [_Granularity Code]
VAR _cmp        = [_Comparator Code]
VAR _sc         = [_Scope Code]
VAR _pshort     = [_Prev Short]
VAR _noun       = [Window Noun]
VAR _rph        = [Region Phrase]
VAR _rev        = [Card Revealed EBITDA]
VAR _pno        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _cact       = [_Category Active]
VAR _cna        = _cact
VAR _nm         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[eb_prev_name] )
VAR _wpk        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period_key] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wq         = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[quarter] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wfy        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[fy] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wps        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[period_key] = _wpk )
VAR _wqs        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy, dim_week[quarter] = _wq )
VAR _wys        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy )
VAR _ps         = IF ( _gr = "W", _wps, _s )
RETURN
    CALCULATE (
        VAR _h1        = CALCULATE ( [EBITDA], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ps, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h2        = CALCULATE ( [EBITDA Bud], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ps, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h3        = CALCULATE ( [Total Margin], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ps, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h4        = CALCULATE ( [Opex Shrink], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ps, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h5        = CALCULATE ( [Opex Store], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ps, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h6        = CALCULATE ( [Opex Central], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ps, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h7        = CALCULATE ( [Opex Fulfilment], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ps, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h8        = CALCULATE ( [EBITDA Margin %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ps, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h9        = CALCULATE ( [Net Sales], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ps, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h10       = CALCULATE ( [Opex Store Bud], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ps, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h11       = CALCULATE ( [Net Sales Bud], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ps, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h12       = CALCULATE ( [EBITDA Margin % Bud], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ps, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h13       = CALCULATE ( [EBITDA Flash], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h14       = CALCULATE ( [EBITDA Flash Bud], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h15       = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _ls ), CALCULATE ( [EBITDA Flash], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ls, dim_week[sort_index] <= _le ) )
        VAR _h16       = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _s2 ), CALCULATE ( [EBITDA Flash], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s2, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e2 ) )
        VAR _v          = _h13
        VAR _b          = _h14
        VAR _c          = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", _h15, "LW", _h16, _b )
        VAR _d          = _v - _c
        VAR _lab        = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", "vs LY", "LW", IF ( _gr = "W", "vs prev. mth", _pshort ), "vs budget" )
        VAR _dtn        = IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ) || ISBLANK ( _c ), "n/a " & _lab, ( VAR _f900003 = _d RETURN IF ( _f900003 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f900003 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900003 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900003 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) & " (" & ( VAR _f900004 = DIVIDE ( _v, _c ) - 1 RETURN IF ( _f900004 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900004 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) & ") " & _lab )
        VAR _dt         = IF ( _cna, "n/a by category", _dtn )
        VAR _bad        = IF ( _b <> 0, 1 - DIVIDE ( _v, _b ) )
        VAR _sw         = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[watch_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "EBITDA" )
        VAR _so         = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[off_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "EBITDA" )
        VAR _rb         = ROUND ( _bad, 10 )
        VAR _st         = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _bad ), SWITCH ( TRUE (), _rb <= _sw, 1, _rb <= _so, 2, 3 ) )
        VAR _val        = IF ( _cna, "–", IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ), "n/a", ( VAR _f900001 = _v RETURN IF ( _f900001 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f900001 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900001 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900001 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) ) ) )
        VAR _unit       = IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ), "", "m" )
        VAR _bt         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _b ), "n/a", ( VAR _f900002 = _b RETURN IF ( _f900002 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f900002 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900002 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900002 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) ) )
        VAR _dlong      = IF ( _cmp = "LW" && _gr = "W" && _nm <> "" && NOT _cna, IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ) || ISBLANK ( _c ), "n/a vs " & _nm, ( VAR _f900005 = _d RETURN IF ( _f900005 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f900005 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900005 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900005 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) & " (" & ( VAR _f900006 = DIVIDE ( _v, _c ) - 1 RETURN IF ( _f900006 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900006 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) & ") vs " & _nm ), _dt )
        VAR _sub        = IF ( _gr = "W", "month " & _pno & " " & "to date", _phrase ) & IF ( LOOKUPVALUE ( dim_kpi[is_provisional], dim_kpi[kpi_code], "EBITDA" ), " · prov.", "" )
        VAR _ink        = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#136C3A", 2, "#9A5B00", 3, "#C0272D", "#5F6878" )
        VAR _cur        = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#136C3A", 2, "#9A5B00", 3, "#C0272D", "#111827" )
        VAR _tint       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#E1F0E5", 2, "#FBEFD6", 3, "#FAE3E3", "#F1F3F5" )
        VAR _chip       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "▲ On track", 2, "● Watch", 3, "▼ Off track", "– N/A" )
        VAR _glyph      = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "▲", 2, "●", 3, "▼", "–" )
        VAR _word       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "On track", 2, "Watch", 3, "Off track", "N/A" )
        VAR _cw         = SWITCH ( _st, 1, 74.8, 2, 63.5, 3, 76.0, 51.2 )
        VAR _sl0        = "EBITDA margin"
        VAR _sv0        = IF ( _cna && _sl0 <> "", "n/a", ( VAR _f247 = _h8 RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f247 ), "n/a", IF ( _f247 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f247 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) ) )
        VAR _sl1        = "Budget margin"
        VAR _sv1        = IF ( _cna && _sl1 <> "", "n/a", ( VAR _f248 = _h12 RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f248 ), "n/a", IF ( _f248 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f248 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) ) )
        VAR _sl2        = "Store costs vs plan"
        VAR _sv2        = IF ( _cna && _sl2 <> "", "n/a", IF ( ISBLANK ( _h5 ) || ISBLANK ( _h9 ) || ISBLANK ( _h10 ) || ISBLANK ( _h11 ) || _h9 = 0 || _h11 = 0, "n/a", ( VAR _f249 = DIVIDE ( _h5, _h9 ) - DIVIDE ( _h10, _h11 ) RETURN IF ( _f249 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f249 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) ) )
        VAR _ex         = IF ( _cna, "Not split by category — OpEx is booked per site and centrally.", ( VAR _f236 = ( _h3 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f236 ), "n/a", IF ( _f236 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f236 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f236 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f236 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & " margin − " & ( VAR _f237 = ( _h4 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f237 ), "n/a", IF ( _f237 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f237 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f237 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f237 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & " shrink − " & ( VAR _f238 = ( _h5 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f238 ), "n/a", IF ( _f238 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f238 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f238 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f238 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & " store − " & ( VAR _f239 = ( _h6 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f239 ), "n/a", IF ( _f239 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f239 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f239 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f239 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & " central − " & ( VAR _f240 = ( _h7 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f240 ), "n/a", IF ( _f240 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f240 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f240 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f240 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & " fulfilment = " & "**" & SUBSTITUTE ( ( VAR _f241 = ( _h1 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( _f241 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f241 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f241 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f241 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ), " ", UNICHAR ( 160 ) ) & "**" & " (" & ( VAR _f242 = _h8 RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f242 ), "n/a", IF ( _f242 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f242 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) ) & " of " & ( VAR _f243 = ( _h9 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f243 ), "n/a", IF ( _f243 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f243 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f243 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f243 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & ")." & IF ( ISBLANK ( _h2 ), "", " Budget " & ( VAR _f244 = _h2 RETURN IF ( _f244 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f244 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f244 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f244 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) & ", so " & ( VAR _f245 = ( _h1 + 0 ) - _h2 RETURN IF ( _f245 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f245 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f245 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f245 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) & " (" & ( VAR _f246 = DIVIDE ( ( _h1 + 0 ), _h2 ) - 1 RETURN IF ( _f246 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f246 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) & ")." ) )
        VAR _footr      = IF ( LOOKUPVALUE ( dim_kpi[is_provisional], dim_kpi[kpi_code], "EBITDA" ), "Provisional · weekly flash", [Updated Text] )
        VAR _pk         =
            SUBSTITUTE ( "Explains Tier 1 · " & _sub, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _dlong, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl0, "|", "/" )

        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_region )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_granularity )
```

**Explain Pack LFL_GROWTH**

```dax
Explain Pack LFL_GROWTH =
VAR _k          = [_Item Key]
VAR _it         = CALCULATETABLE ( dim_item, REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item ), dim_item[item_key] = _k )
VAR _s          = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[start_index] )
VAR _e          = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[end_index] )
VAR _ls         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[ly_start] )
VAR _le         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[ly_end] )
VAR _s2         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[prev_start] )
VAR _e2         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[prev_end] )
VAR _iso        = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[sort_order] )
VAR _phrase     = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[phrase] )
VAR _gr         = [_Granularity Code]
VAR _cmp        = [_Comparator Code]
VAR _sc         = [_Scope Code]
VAR _pshort     = [_Prev Short]
VAR _noun       = [Window Noun]
VAR _rph        = [Region Phrase]
VAR _rev        = [Card Revealed LFL_GROWTH]
VAR _pno        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _cact       = [_Category Active]
VAR _cna        = FALSE ()
VAR _wpk        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period_key] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wq         = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[quarter] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wfy        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[fy] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wps        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[period_key] = _wpk )
VAR _wqs        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy, dim_week[quarter] = _wq )
VAR _wys        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy )
VAR _ps         = IF ( _gr = "W", _wps, _s )
RETURN
    CALCULATE (
        VAR _h1        = CALCULATE ( [LFL Growth %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h2        = CALCULATE ( [LFL Growth % Bud], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h3        = CALCULATE ( [LFL Store Count], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h4        = CALCULATE ( [LFL Net Sales], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h5        = CALCULATE ( [LFL Net Sales LY], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h6        = CALCULATE ( [Mix Online Net Sales], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h7        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _ls ), CALCULATE ( [Mix Online Net Sales], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ls, dim_week[sort_index] <= _le ) )
        VAR _h8        = CALCULATE ( [Trading Store Count], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h9        = CALCULATE ( [Non-LFL Effect pp], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h10       = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _ls ), CALCULATE ( [LFL Growth %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ls, dim_week[sort_index] <= _le ) )
        VAR _h11       = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _s2 ), CALCULATE ( [LFL Growth %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s2, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e2 ) )
        VAR _v          = _h1
        VAR _b          = _h2
        VAR _c          = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", _h10, "LW", _h11, _b )
        VAR _d          = _v - _c
        VAR _lab        = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", "vs LY", "LW", _pshort, "vs budget LFL" )
        VAR _dt         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ) || ISBLANK ( _c ), "n/a " & _lab, ( VAR _f900006 = _d RETURN IF ( _f900006 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900006 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) & " " & _lab & " " & IF ( _cmp = "BUD", ( VAR _f900005 = _c RETURN IF ( _f900005 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900005 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ), ( VAR _f900007 = _c RETURN IF ( _f900007 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900007 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) ) )
        VAR _bad        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _v ) && NOT ISBLANK ( _b ), _b - _v )
        VAR _sw         = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[watch_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "LFL_GROWTH" )
        VAR _so         = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[off_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "LFL_GROWTH" )
        VAR _rb         = ROUND ( _bad, 10 )
        VAR _st         = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _bad ), SWITCH ( TRUE (), _rb <= _sw, 1, _rb <= _so, 2, 3 ) )
        VAR _val        = IF ( _cna, "–", IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ), "n/a", SUBSTITUTE ( ( VAR _f900002 = _v RETURN IF ( _f900002 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900002 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ), "%", "" ) ) )
        VAR _unit       = IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ), "", "%" )
        VAR _bt         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _b ), "n/a", SUBSTITUTE ( ( VAR _f900004 = _b RETURN IF ( _f900004 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900004 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ), "%", "" ) )
        VAR _dlong      = _dt
        VAR _sub        = "vs LY" & " " & _noun & IF ( LOOKUPVALUE ( dim_kpi[is_provisional], dim_kpi[kpi_code], "LFL_GROWTH" ), " · prov.", "" )
        VAR _ink        = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#136C3A", 2, "#9A5B00", 3, "#C0272D", "#5F6878" )
        VAR _cur        = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#136C3A", 2, "#9A5B00", 3, "#C0272D", "#111827" )
        VAR _tint       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#E1F0E5", 2, "#FBEFD6", 3, "#FAE3E3", "#F1F3F5" )
        VAR _chip       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "▲ On track", 2, "● Watch", 3, "▼ Off track", "– N/A" )
        VAR _glyph      = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "▲", 2, "●", 3, "▼", "–" )
        VAR _word       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "On track", 2, "Watch", 3, "Off track", "N/A" )
        VAR _cw         = SWITCH ( _st, 1, 74.8, 2, 63.5, 3, 76.0, 51.2 )
        VAR _sl0        = "LFL stores"
        VAR _sv0        = IF ( _cna && _sl0 <> "", "n/a", IF ( ISBLANK ( _h1 ), "n/a", ( VAR _f250 = ( _h3 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f250 ), "n/a", IF ( _f250 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f250 ), "#,0" ) ) ) & " of " & ( VAR _f256 = ( _h8 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f256 ), "n/a", IF ( _f256 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f256 ), "#,0" ) ) ) ) )
        VAR _sl1        = "Non-LFL effect"
        VAR _sv1        = IF ( _cna && _sl1 <> "", "n/a", ( VAR _f257 = _h9 RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f257 ), "n/a", IF ( _f257 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f257 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) ) )
        VAR _sl2        = "Online LFL"
        VAR _sv2        = IF ( _cna && _sl2 <> "", "n/a", IF ( ISBLANK ( _h7 ) || _h7 = 0, "n/a", ( VAR _f258 = DIVIDE ( _h6, _h7 ) - 1 RETURN IF ( _f258 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f258 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) ) )
        VAR _ex         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _h1 ), "Like-for-like counts stores only; this selection has none.", ( VAR _f250 = ( _h3 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f250 ), "n/a", IF ( _f250 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f250 ), "#,0" ) ) ) & " LFL stores: " & ( VAR _f251 = ( _h4 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f251 ), "n/a", IF ( _f251 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f251 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f251 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f251 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & " ÷ " & ( VAR _f252 = ( _h5 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f252 ), "n/a", IF ( _f252 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f252 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f252 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f252 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & " − 1 = " & "**" & SUBSTITUTE ( ( VAR _f253 = _h1 RETURN IF ( _f253 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f253 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ), " ", UNICHAR ( 160 ) ) & "**" & IF ( ISBLANK ( _h2 ), ".", ". Budget LFL " & ( VAR _f254 = _h2 RETURN IF ( _f254 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f254 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) & ", so " & ( VAR _f255 = _h1 - _h2 RETURN IF ( _f255 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f255 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) & "." ) )
        VAR _footr      = [Updated Text]
        VAR _pk         =
            SUBSTITUTE ( "Explains Tier 1 · " & _sub, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _dlong, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl0, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv0, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl1, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv1, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl2, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv2, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _ex, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _footr, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( "Drill path · Sales page", "|", "/" )
        RETURN
            _pk
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_region )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_granularity )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_comparator )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_group )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_lever )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_owner )
        ,FILTER ( ALL ( dim_store[scope_code] ), _sc = "ALL" || dim_store[scope_code] = _sc )
    )
```

**Explain Pack FRONT_GM**

```dax
Explain Pack FRONT_GM =
VAR _k          = [_Item Key]
VAR _it         = CALCULATETABLE ( dim_item, REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item ), dim_item[item_key] = _k )
VAR _s          = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[start_index] )
VAR _e          = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[end_index] )
VAR _ls         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[ly_start] )
VAR _le         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[ly_end] )
VAR _s2         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[prev_start] )
VAR _e2         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[prev_end] )
VAR _iso        = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[sort_order] )
VAR _phrase     = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[phrase] )
VAR _gr         = [_Granularity Code]
VAR _cmp        = [_Comparator Code]
VAR _sc         = [_Scope Code]
VAR _pshort     = [_Prev Short]
VAR _noun       = [Window Noun]
VAR _rph        = [Region Phrase]
VAR _rev        = [Card Revealed FRONT_GM]
VAR _pno        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _cact       = [_Category Active]
VAR _cna        = FALSE ()
VAR _wpk        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period_key] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wq         = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[quarter] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wfy        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[fy] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wps        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[period_key] = _wpk )
VAR _wqs        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy, dim_week[quarter] = _wq )
VAR _wys        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy )
VAR _ps         = IF ( _gr = "W", _wps, _s )
RETURN
    CALCULATE (
        VAR _h1        = CALCULATE ( [Front GM %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h2        = CALCULATE ( [Front GM % Bud], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h3        = CALCULATE ( [Net Sales], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h4        = CALCULATE ( [COGS], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h5        = CALCULATE ( [Front Margin], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h6        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _ls ), CALCULATE ( [Front GM %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ls, dim_week[sort_index] <= _le ) )
        VAR _h7        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _s2 ), CALCULATE ( [Front GM %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s2, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e2 ) )
        VAR _v          = _h1
        VAR _b          = _h2
        VAR _c          = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", _h6, "LW", _h7, _b )
        VAR _d          = _v - _c
        VAR _lab        = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", "vs LY", "LW", _pshort, "vs budget" )
        VAR _dt         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ) || ISBLANK ( _c ), "n/a " & _lab, ( VAR _f900004 = _d RETURN IF ( _f900004 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900004 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) & " " & _lab & " " & IF ( _cmp = "BUD", ( VAR _f900003 = _c RETURN IF ( _f900003 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900003 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ), ( VAR _f900005 = _c RETURN IF ( _f900005 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900005 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) ) )
        VAR _bad        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _v ) && NOT ISBLANK ( _b ), _b - _v )
        VAR _sw         = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[watch_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "FRONT_GM" )
        VAR _so         = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[off_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "FRONT_GM" )
        VAR _rb         = ROUND ( _bad, 10 )
        VAR _st         = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _bad ), SWITCH ( TRUE (), _rb <= _sw, 1, _rb <= _so, 2, 3 ) )
        VAR _val        = IF ( _cna, "–", IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ), "n/a", ( VAR _f900001 = _v RETURN IF ( _f900001 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900001 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) ) ) )
        VAR _unit       = IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ), "", "%" )
        VAR _bt         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _b ), "n/a", ( VAR _f900002 = _b RETURN IF ( _f900002 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900002 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) ) )
        VAR _dlong      = _dt
        VAR _sub        = "% of net sales" & IF ( LOOKUPVALUE ( dim_kpi[is_provisional], dim_kpi[kpi_code], "FRONT_GM" ), " · prov.", "" )
        VAR _ink        = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#136C3A", 2, "#9A5B00", 3, "#C0272D", "#5F6878" )
        VAR _cur        = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#136C3A", 2, "#9A5B00", 3, "#C0272D", "#111827" )
        VAR _tint       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#E1F0E5", 2, "#FBEFD6", 3, "#FAE3E3", "#F1F3F5" )
        VAR _chip       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "▲ On track", 2, "● Watch", 3, "▼ Off track", "– N/A" )
        VAR _glyph      = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "▲", 2, "●", 3, "▼", "–" )
        VAR _word       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "On track", 2, "Watch", 3, "Off track", "N/A" )
        VAR _cw         = SWITCH ( _st, 1, 74.8, 2, 63.5, 3, 76.0, 51.2 )
        VAR _sl0        = "Cost of goods"
        VAR _sv0        = IF ( _cna && _sl0 <> "", "n/a", ( VAR _f265 = ( _h4 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f265 ), "n/a", IF ( _f265 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f265 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f265 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f265 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) )
        VAR _sl1        = "Front margin"
        VAR _sv1        = IF ( _cna && _sl1 <> "", "n/a", ( VAR _f266 = ( _h5 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f266 ), "n/a", IF ( _f266 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f266 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f266 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f266 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) )
        VAR _sl2        = "vs LY " & _noun
        VAR _sv2        = IF ( _cna && _sl2 <> "", "n/a", IF ( ISBLANK ( _h1 ) || ISBLANK ( _h6 ), "n/a", ( VAR _f267 = _h1 - _h6 RETURN IF ( _f267 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f267 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) ) )
        VAR _ex         = ( VAR _f259 = ( _h3 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f259 ), "n/a", IF ( _f259 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f259 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f259 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f259 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & " − " & ( VAR _f260 = ( _h4 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f260 ), "n/a", IF ( _f260 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f260 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f260 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f260 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & " cost of goods = " & ( VAR _f261 = ( _h5 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f261 ), "n/a", IF ( _f261 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f261 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f261 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f261 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & "; ÷ " & ( VAR _f259 = ( _h3 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f259 ), "n/a", IF ( _f259 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f259 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f259 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f259 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & " = " & "**" & SUBSTITUTE ( ( VAR _f262 = _h1 RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f262 ), "n/a", IF ( _f262 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f262 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) ), " ", UNICHAR ( 160 ) ) & "**" & IF ( ISBLANK ( _h1 ) || ISBLANK ( _h2 ), ".", ". Budget " & ( VAR _f263 = _h2 RETURN IF ( _f263 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f263 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) & ", so " & ( VAR _f264 = _h1 - _h2 RETURN IF ( _f264 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f264 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) & "." )
        VAR _footr      = [Updated Text]
        VAR _pk         =
            SUBSTITUTE ( "Explains Tier 1 · " & _sub, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _dlong, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl0, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv0, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl1, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv1, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl2, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv2, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _ex, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _footr, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( "Drill path · Margin page", "|", "/" )
        RETURN
            _pk
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_region )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_granularity )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_comparator )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_group )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_lever )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_owner )
        ,FILTER ( ALL ( dim_store[scope_code] ), _sc = "ALL" || dim_store[scope_code] = _sc )
    )
```
