**Explain Pack SELL_THROUGH**

```dax
Explain Pack SELL_THROUGH =
VAR _k          = [_Item Key]
VAR _it         = CALCULATETABLE ( dim_item, REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item ), dim_item[item_key] = _k )
VAR _s          = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[start_index] )
VAR _e          = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[end_index] )
VAR _ls         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[ly_start] )
VAR _le         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[ly_end] )
VAR _s2         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[prev_start] )
VAR _e2         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[prev_end] )
VAR _iso        = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[sort_order] )
VAR _phrase     = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[phrase] )
VAR _gr         = [_Granularity Code]
VAR _cmp        = [_Comparator Code]
VAR _sc         = [_Scope Code]
VAR _pshort     = [_Prev Short]
VAR _noun       = [Window Noun]
VAR _rph        = [Region Phrase]
VAR _rev        = [Card Revealed SELL_THROUGH]
VAR _pno        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _cact       = [_Category Active]
VAR _cna        = _cact && NOT ( TRUE () IN VALUES ( dim_category[is_seasonal] ) )
VAR _so1        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_category[sort_order] ), dim_category[is_seasonal] = TRUE () )
VAR _so2        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_category[sort_order] ), dim_category[is_seasonal] = TRUE (), dim_category[sort_order] > _so1 )
VAR _pc1        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_category[category_code] ), dim_category[sort_order] = _so1 )
VAR _pc2        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_category[category_code] ), dim_category[sort_order] = _so2 )
VAR _pn1        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_category[category_name] ), dim_category[sort_order] = _so1 )
VAR _pn2        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_category[category_name] ), dim_category[sort_order] = _so2 )
VAR _pk         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _so1 ), 0, IF ( ISBLANK ( _so2 ), 1, 2 ) )
VAR _wpk        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period_key] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wq         = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[quarter] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wfy        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[fy] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wps        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[period_key] = _wpk )
VAR _wqs        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy, dim_week[quarter] = _wq )
VAR _wys        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy )
VAR _ps         = IF ( _gr = "W", _wps, _s )
RETURN
    CALCULATE (
        VAR _h1        = CALCULATE ( [Sell-through %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h2        = CALCULATE ( [Sell-through Target], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h3        = CALCULATE ( [Season Opening Units], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h4        = CALCULATE ( [Season Receipt Units], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h5        = CALCULATE ( [Season Units Sold], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h6        = CALCULATE ( CALCULATE ( [Sell-through %], dim_category[category_code] = _pc1 ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h7        = CALCULATE ( CALCULATE ( [Sell-through %], dim_category[category_code] = _pc2 ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h8        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _ls ), CALCULATE ( [Sell-through %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ls, dim_week[sort_index] <= _le ) )
        VAR _h9        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _s2 ), CALCULATE ( [Sell-through %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s2, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e2 ) )
        VAR _v          = _h1
        VAR _b          = _h2
        VAR _c          = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", _h8, "LW", _h9, _b )
        VAR _d          = _v - _c
        VAR _lab        = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", "vs LY", "LW", _pshort, "vs target" )
        VAR _dtn        = IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ) || ISBLANK ( _c ), "n/a " & _lab, ( VAR _f900005 = _d RETURN IF ( _f900005 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900005 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) & " " & _lab & " " & IF ( _cmp = "BUD", ( VAR _f900003 = _c RETURN ( VAR _f900004 = FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900003 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) RETURN IF ( RIGHT ( _f900004, 2 ) = ".0", LEFT ( _f900004, LEN ( _f900004 ) - 2 ), _f900004 ) ) & "%" ), ( VAR _f900006 = _c RETURN IF ( _f900006 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900006 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) ) )
        VAR _dt         = IF ( _cna, "n/a by category", _dtn )
        VAR _bad        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _v ), _b - _v )
        VAR _sw         = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[watch_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "SELL_THROUGH" )
        VAR _so         = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[off_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "SELL_THROUGH" )
        VAR _rb         = ROUND ( _bad, 10 )
        VAR _st         = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _bad ), SWITCH ( TRUE (), _rb <= _sw, 1, _rb <= _so, 2, 3 ) )
        VAR _val        = IF ( _cna, "–", IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ), "n/a", ( VAR _f900001 = _v RETURN IF ( _f900001 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900001 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) ) ) )
        VAR _unit       = IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ), "", "%" )
        VAR _bt         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _b ), "n/a", ( VAR _f900002 = _b RETURN IF ( _f900002 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900002 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) ) )
        VAR _dlong      = _dt
        VAR _sub        = "A/W season to date" & IF ( LOOKUPVALUE ( dim_kpi[is_provisional], dim_kpi[kpi_code], "SELL_THROUGH" ), " · prov.", "" )
        VAR _ink        = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#136C3A", 2, "#9A5B00", 3, "#C0272D", "#5F6878" )
        VAR _cur        = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#136C3A", 2, "#9A5B00", 3, "#C0272D", "#111827" )
        VAR _tint       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#E1F0E5", 2, "#FBEFD6", 3, "#FAE3E3", "#F1F3F5" )
        VAR _chip       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "▲ On track", 2, "● Watch", 3, "▼ Off track", "– N/A" )
        VAR _glyph      = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "▲", 2, "●", 3, "▼", "–" )
        VAR _word       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "On track", 2, "Watch", 3, "Off track", "N/A" )
        VAR _cw         = SWITCH ( _st, 1, 74.8, 2, 63.5, 3, 76.0, 51.2 )
        VAR _sl0        = IF ( _pk >= 1, _pn1, "vs LY season" )
        VAR _sv0        = IF ( _cna && _sl0 <> "", "n/a", IF ( _pk >= 1, ( VAR _f276 = _h6 RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f276 ), "n/a", IF ( _f276 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f276 * 100 ), "#,0" ) & "%" ) ), IF ( ISBLANK ( _h1 ), "n/a", IF ( ISBLANK ( _h1 ) || ISBLANK ( _h8 ), "n/a", ( VAR _f278 = _h1 - _h8 RETURN IF ( _f278 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f278 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) ) ) ) )
        VAR _sl1        = IF ( _pk >= 2, _pn2, IF ( _pk = 1, "vs LY season", "" ) )
        VAR _sv1        = IF ( _cna && _sl1 <> "", "n/a", IF ( _pk >= 2, ( VAR _f277 = _h7 RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f277 ), "n/a", IF ( _f277 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f277 * 100 ), "#,0" ) & "%" ) ), IF ( _pk = 1, IF ( ISBLANK ( _h1 ), "n/a", IF ( ISBLANK ( _h1 ) || ISBLANK ( _h8 ), "n/a", ( VAR _f278 = _h1 - _h8 RETURN IF ( _f278 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f278 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) ) ), "" ) ) )
        VAR _sl2        = IF ( _pk >= 2, "vs LY season", "" )
        VAR _sv2        = IF ( _cna && _sl2 <> "", "n/a", IF ( _pk >= 2, IF ( ISBLANK ( _h1 ), "n/a", IF ( ISBLANK ( _h1 ) || ISBLANK ( _h8 ), "n/a", ( VAR _f278 = _h1 - _h8 RETURN IF ( _f278 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f278 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) ) ), "" ) )
        VAR _ex         = IF ( _cna, "No seasonal category in the selection.", IF ( ISBLANK ( _h1 ) || ISBLANK ( _h2 ), IF ( _gr = "W", "No season is open in the selected week.", "No season is open in the last week of the " & _noun & "." ), ( VAR _f268 = ( _h5 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f268 ), "n/a", FORMAT ( _f268 / 1000000, "#,0.0" ) & "m" ) ) & " units sold ÷ (" & ( VAR _f269 = ( _h3 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f269 ), "n/a", FORMAT ( _f269 / 1000000, "#,0.0" ) & "m" ) ) & " opening + " & ( VAR _f270 = ( _h4 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f270 ), "n/a", FORMAT ( _f270 / 1000000, "#,0.0" ) & "m" ) ) & " received = " & ( VAR _f271 = ( _h3 + 0 ) + ( _h4 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f271 ), "n/a", FORMAT ( _f271 / 1000000, "#,0.0" ) & "m" ) ) & ") = " & "**" & SUBSTITUTE ( ( VAR _f272 = _h1 RETURN IF ( _f272 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f272 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ), " ", UNICHAR ( 160 ) ) & "**" & ". Target " & ( VAR _f273 = _h2 RETURN ( VAR _f274 = FORMAT ( ABS ( _f273 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) RETURN IF ( RIGHT ( _f274, 2 ) = ".0", LEFT ( _f274, LEN ( _f274 ) - 2 ), _f274 ) ) & "%" ) & " at week " & IF ( ISBLANK ( CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[season_week] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e ) ), "n/a", FORMAT ( CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[season_week] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e ), "0" ) ) & " of " & FORMAT ( ( VAR _sc = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[season_code] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e ) RETURN CALCULATE ( COUNTROWS ( dim_week ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[season_code] = _sc ) ), "0" ) & ", so " & ( VAR _f275 = _h1 - _h2 RETURN IF ( _f275 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f275 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) & "." & IF ( _cact, " Seasonal categories only.", "" ) ) )
        VAR _footr      = "Updated weekly"
        VAR _pk         =
            SUBSTITUTE ( "Explains Tier 1 · " & _sub, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _dlong, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl0, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv0, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl1, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv1, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl2, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv2, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _ex, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _footr, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( "Drill path · Inventory page", "|", "/" )
        RETURN
            _pk
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_region )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_granularity )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_comparator )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_group )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_lever )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_owner )
        ,FILTER ( ALL ( dim_store[scope_code] ), _sc = "ALL" || dim_store[scope_code] = _sc )
    )
```

**Explain Pack GMROI**

```dax
Explain Pack GMROI =
VAR _k          = [_Item Key]
VAR _it         = CALCULATETABLE ( dim_item, REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item ), dim_item[item_key] = _k )
VAR _s          = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[start_index] )
VAR _e          = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[end_index] )
VAR _ls         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[ly_start] )
VAR _le         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[ly_end] )
VAR _s2         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[prev_start] )
VAR _e2         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[prev_end] )
VAR _iso        = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[sort_order] )
VAR _phrase     = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[phrase] )
VAR _gr         = [_Granularity Code]
VAR _cmp        = [_Comparator Code]
VAR _sc         = [_Scope Code]
VAR _pshort     = [_Prev Short]
VAR _noun       = [Window Noun]
VAR _rph        = [Region Phrase]
VAR _rev        = [Card Revealed GMROI]
VAR _pno        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _cact       = [_Category Active]
VAR _cna        = FALSE ()
VAR _so1        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_category[sort_order] ) )
VAR _so2        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_category[sort_order] ), dim_category[sort_order] > _so1 )
VAR _pc1        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_category[category_code] ), dim_category[sort_order] = _so1 )
VAR _pc2        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_category[category_code] ), dim_category[sort_order] = _so2 )
VAR _pn1        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_category[category_name] ), dim_category[sort_order] = _so1 )
VAR _pn2        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_category[category_name] ), dim_category[sort_order] = _so2 )
VAR _pk         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _so1 ), 0, IF ( ISBLANK ( _so2 ), 1, 2 ) )
VAR _wpk        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period_key] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wq         = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[quarter] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wfy        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[fy] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wps        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[period_key] = _wpk )
VAR _wqs        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy, dim_week[quarter] = _wq )
VAR _wys        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy )
VAR _ps         = IF ( _gr = "W", _wps, _s )
RETURN
    CALCULATE (
        VAR _h1        = CALCULATE ( [GMROI], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h2        = CALCULATE ( [GMROI Target], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h3        = CALCULATE ( [GMROI Front Margin R52W], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h4        = CALCULATE ( [GMROI Avg On Hand Cost], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h5        = CALCULATE ( CALCULATE ( [GMROI], dim_category[category_code] = _pc1 ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h6        = CALCULATE ( CALCULATE ( [GMROI], dim_category[category_code] = _pc2 ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h7        = CALCULATE ( CALCULATE ( [GMROI], dim_category[category_code] = "HB" ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h8        = CALCULATE ( CALCULATE ( [GMROI], dim_category[category_code] = "ELEC" ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h9        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _ls ), CALCULATE ( [GMROI], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ls, dim_week[sort_index] <= _le ) )
        VAR _h10       = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _s2 ), CALCULATE ( [GMROI], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s2, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e2 ) )
        VAR _v          = _h1
        VAR _b          = _h2
        VAR _c          = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", _h9, "LW", _h10, _b )
        VAR _d          = _v - _c
        VAR _lab        = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", "vs LY", "LW", _pshort, "vs target" )
        VAR _dt         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ) || ISBLANK ( _c ), "n/a " & _lab, ( VAR _f900003 = _d RETURN IF ( _f900003 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900003 ), "#,0.0" ) & "×" ) & " " & _lab & " " & ( VAR _f900004 = _c RETURN IF ( _f900004 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900004 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "×" )
        VAR _bad        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _v ), _b - _v )
        VAR _sw         = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[watch_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "GMROI" )
        VAR _so         = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[off_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "GMROI" )
        VAR _rb         = ROUND ( _bad, 10 )
        VAR _st         = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _bad ), SWITCH ( TRUE (), _rb <= _sw, 1, _rb <= _so, 2, 3 ) )
        VAR _val        = IF ( _cna, "–", IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ), "n/a", ( VAR _f900001 = _v RETURN IF ( _f900001 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900001 ), "#,0.0" ) ) ) )
        VAR _unit       = IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ), "", "×" )
        VAR _bt         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _b ), "n/a", ( VAR _f900002 = _b RETURN IF ( _f900002 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900002 ), "#,0.0" ) ) )
        VAR _dlong      = _dt
        VAR _sub        = "rolling 12 months" & IF ( LOOKUPVALUE ( dim_kpi[is_provisional], dim_kpi[kpi_code], "GMROI" ), " · prov.", "" )
        VAR _ink        = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#136C3A", 2, "#9A5B00", 3, "#C0272D", "#5F6878" )
        VAR _cur        = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#136C3A", 2, "#9A5B00", 3, "#C0272D", "#111827" )
        VAR _tint       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#E1F0E5", 2, "#FBEFD6", 3, "#FAE3E3", "#F1F3F5" )
        VAR _chip       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "▲ On track", 2, "● Watch", 3, "▼ Off track", "– N/A" )
        VAR _glyph      = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "▲", 2, "●", 3, "▼", "–" )
        VAR _word       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "On track", 2, "Watch", 3, "Off track", "N/A" )
        VAR _cw         = SWITCH ( _st, 1, 74.8, 2, 63.5, 3, 76.0, 51.2 )
        VAR _sl0        = "R52W front margin"
        VAR _sv0        = IF ( _cna && _sl0 <> "", "n/a", ( VAR _f288 = ( _h3 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f288 ), "n/a", IF ( _f288 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f288 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ) & "m" ) ) )
        VAR _sl1        = "Average stock at cost"
        VAR _sv1        = IF ( _cna && _sl1 <> "", "n/a", ( VAR _f289 = ( _h4 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f289 ), "n/a", IF ( _f289 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f289 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ) & "m" ) ) )
        VAR _sl2        = IF ( _cact, _pn1 & IF ( _pk >= 2, " · " & _pn2, "" ), "H&B · Electronics" )
        VAR _sv2        = IF ( _cna && _sl2 <> "", "n/a", IF ( _cact, ( VAR _f284 = _h5 RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f284 ), "n/a", IF ( _f284 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f284 ), "#,0.0" ) ) ) & "×" & IF ( _pk >= 2, " · " & ( VAR _f285 = _h6 RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f285 ), "n/a", IF ( _f285 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f285 ), "#,0.0" ) ) ) & "×", "" ), ( VAR _f286 = _h7 RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f286 ), "n/a", IF ( _f286 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f286 ), "#,0.0" ) ) ) & "×" & " · " & ( VAR _f287 = _h8 RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f287 ), "n/a", IF ( _f287 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f287 ), "#,0.0" ) ) ) & "×" ) )
        VAR _ex         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _h1 ) || ISBLANK ( _h2 ), "GMROI needs 52 weeks of history.", ( VAR _f279 = ( _h3 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f279 ), "n/a", IF ( _f279 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f279 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ) & "m" ) ) & " front margin ÷ " & ( VAR _f280 = ( _h4 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f280 ), "n/a", IF ( _f280 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f280 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ) & "m" ) ) & " average stock = " & "**" & SUBSTITUTE ( ( VAR _f281 = _h1 RETURN IF ( _f281 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f281 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "×", " ", UNICHAR ( 160 ) ) & "**" & ". Target " & ( VAR _f282 = _h2 RETURN IF ( _f282 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f282 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "×, so " & ( VAR _f283 = _h1 - _h2 RETURN IF ( _f283 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f283 ), "#,0.0" ) & "×" ) & "." )
        VAR _footr      = "Updated weekly"
        VAR _pk         =
            SUBSTITUTE ( "Explains Tier 1 · " & _sub, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _dlong, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl0, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv0, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl1, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv1, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl2, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv2, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _ex, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _footr, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( "Drill path · Inventory page", "|", "/" )
        RETURN
            _pk
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_region )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_granularity )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_comparator )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_group )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_lever )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_owner )
        ,FILTER ( ALL ( dim_store[scope_code] ), _sc = "ALL" || dim_store[scope_code] = _sc )
    )
```

**Explain Pack SUPPLIER_OTIF**

```dax
Explain Pack SUPPLIER_OTIF =
VAR _k          = [_Item Key]
VAR _it         = CALCULATETABLE ( dim_item, REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item ), dim_item[item_key] = _k )
VAR _s          = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[start_index] )
VAR _e          = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[end_index] )
VAR _ls         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[ly_start] )
VAR _le         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[ly_end] )
VAR _s2         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[prev_start] )
VAR _e2         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[prev_end] )
VAR _iso        = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[sort_order] )
VAR _phrase     = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[phrase] )
VAR _gr         = [_Granularity Code]
VAR _cmp        = [_Comparator Code]
VAR _sc         = [_Scope Code]
VAR _pshort     = [_Prev Short]
VAR _noun       = [Window Noun]
VAR _rph        = [Region Phrase]
VAR _rev        = [Card Revealed SUPPLIER_OTIF]
VAR _pno        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _cact       = [_Category Active]
VAR _cna        = _cact
VAR _wpk        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period_key] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wq         = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[quarter] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wfy        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[fy] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wps        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[period_key] = _wpk )
VAR _wqs        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy, dim_week[quarter] = _wq )
VAR _wys        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy )
VAR _ps         = IF ( _gr = "W", _wps, _s )
RETURN
    CALCULATE (
        VAR _h1        = CALCULATE ( [OTIF %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h2        = CALCULATE ( [OTIF Target], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h3        = CALCULATE ( [PO Lines OTIF], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h4        = CALCULATE ( [PO Lines Due], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h5        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _s2 ), CALCULATE ( [OTIF %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s2, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e2 ) )
        VAR _h6        = CALCULATE ( [OTIF Suppliers Below 85], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
        VAR _h7        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _ls ), CALCULATE ( [OTIF %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ls, dim_week[sort_index] <= _le ) )
        VAR _v          = _h1
        VAR _b          = _h2
        VAR _c          = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", _h7, "LW", _h5, _b )
        VAR _d          = _v - _c
        VAR _lab        = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", "vs LY", "LW", _pshort, "vs target" )
        VAR _dtn        = IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ) || ISBLANK ( _c ), "n/a " & _lab, ( VAR _f900005 = _d RETURN IF ( _f900005 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900005 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) & " " & _lab & " " & IF ( _cmp = "BUD", ( VAR _f900003 = _c RETURN ( VAR _f900004 = FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900003 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) RETURN IF ( RIGHT ( _f900004, 2 ) = ".0", LEFT ( _f900004, LEN ( _f900004 ) - 2 ), _f900004 ) ) & "%" ), ( VAR _f900006 = _c RETURN IF ( _f900006 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900006 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) ) )
        VAR _dt         = IF ( _cna, "n/a by category", _dtn )
        VAR _bad        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _v ), _b - _v )
        VAR _sw         = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[watch_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "SUPPLIER_OTIF" )
        VAR _so         = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[off_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "SUPPLIER_OTIF" )
        VAR _rb         = ROUND ( _bad, 10 )
        VAR _st         = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _bad ), SWITCH ( TRUE (), _rb <= _sw, 1, _rb <= _so, 2, 3 ) )
        VAR _val        = IF ( _cna, "–", IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ), "n/a", ( VAR _f900001 = _v RETURN IF ( _f900001 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900001 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) ) ) )
        VAR _unit       = IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ), "", "%" )
        VAR _bt         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _b ), "n/a", ( VAR _f900002 = _b RETURN IF ( _f900002 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900002 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) ) )
        VAR _dlong      = _dt
        VAR _sub        = "inbound, PO lines" & IF ( LOOKUPVALUE ( dim_kpi[is_provisional], dim_kpi[kpi_code], "SUPPLIER_OTIF" ), " · prov.", "" )
        VAR _ink        = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#136C3A", 2, "#9A5B00", 3, "#C0272D", "#5F6878" )
        VAR _cur        = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#136C3A", 2, "#9A5B00", 3, "#C0272D", "#111827" )
        VAR _tint       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#E1F0E5", 2, "#FBEFD6", 3, "#FAE3E3", "#F1F3F5" )
        VAR _chip       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "▲ On track", 2, "● Watch", 3, "▼ Off track", "– N/A" )
        VAR _glyph      = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "▲", 2, "●", 3, "▼", "–" )
        VAR _word       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "On track", 2, "Watch", 3, "Off track", "N/A" )
        VAR _cw         = SWITCH ( _st, 1, 74.8, 2, 63.5, 3, 76.0, 51.2 )
        VAR _sl0        = "PO lines due"
        VAR _sv0        = IF ( _cna && _sl0 <> "", "n/a", ( VAR _f296 = ( _h4 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f296 ), "n/a", IF ( _f296 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f296 ), "#,0" ) ) ) )
        VAR _sl1        = IF ( _gr = "W", "vs last week", _pshort )
        VAR _sv1        = IF ( _cna && _sl1 <> "", "n/a", IF ( ISBLANK ( _h1 ) || ISBLANK ( _h5 ), "n/a", ( VAR _f297 = _h1 - _h5 RETURN IF ( _f297 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f297 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) ) )
        VAR _sl2        = "Suppliers below 85%"
        VAR _sv2        = IF ( _cna && _sl2 <> "", "n/a", ( VAR _f298 = ( _h6 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f298 ), "n/a", IF ( _f298 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f298 ), "#,0" ) ) ) )
        VAR _ex         = IF ( _cna, "Not split by category — PO lines are per supplier.", ( VAR _f290 = ( _h3 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f290 ), "n/a", IF ( _f290 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f290 ), "#,0" ) ) ) & " lines on time and in full ÷ " & ( VAR _f291 = ( _h4 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f291 ), "n/a", IF ( _f291 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f291 ), "#,0" ) ) ) & " lines due = " & "**" & SUBSTITUTE ( ( VAR _f292 = ( _h1 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( _f292 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f292 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ), " ", UNICHAR ( 160 ) ) & "**" & ". Target " & ( VAR _f293 = ( _h2 + 0 ) RETURN ( VAR _f294 = FORMAT ( ABS ( _f293 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) RETURN IF ( RIGHT ( _f294, 2 ) = ".0", LEFT ( _f294, LEN ( _f294 ) - 2 ), _f294 ) ) & "%" ) & ", so " & ( VAR _f295 = ( _h1 + 0 ) - ( _h2 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( _f295 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f295 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) & "." )
        VAR _footr      = [Updated Text]
        VAR _pk         =
            SUBSTITUTE ( "Explains Tier 1 · " & _sub, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _dlong, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl0, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv0, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl1, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv1, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl2, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv2, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _ex, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _footr, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( "Drill path · Supplier page", "|", "/" )
        RETURN
            _pk
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_region )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_granularity )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_comparator )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_group )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_lever )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_owner )
        ,FILTER ( ALL ( dim_store[scope_code] ), _sc = "ALL" || dim_store[scope_code] = _sc )
    )
```

**Explain Pack CHANNEL_MIX**

```dax
Explain Pack CHANNEL_MIX =
VAR _k          = [_Item Key]
VAR _it         = CALCULATETABLE ( dim_item, REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item ), dim_item[item_key] = _k )
VAR _s          = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[start_index] )
VAR _e          = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[end_index] )
VAR _ls         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[ly_start] )
VAR _le         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[ly_end] )
VAR _s2         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[prev_start] )
VAR _e2         = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[prev_end] )
VAR _iso        = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[sort_order] )
VAR _phrase     = MAXX ( _it, dim_item[phrase] )
VAR _gr         = [_Granularity Code]
VAR _cmp        = [_Comparator Code]
VAR _sc         = [_Scope Code]
VAR _pshort     = [_Prev Short]
VAR _noun       = [Window Noun]
VAR _rph        = [Region Phrase]
VAR _rev        = [Card Revealed CHANNEL_MIX]
VAR _pno        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _cact       = [_Category Active]
VAR _cna        = FALSE ()
VAR _wpk        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[period_key] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wq         = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[quarter] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wfy        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[fy] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _e )
VAR _wps        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[period_key] = _wpk )
VAR _wqs        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy, dim_week[quarter] = _wq )
VAR _wys        = CALCULATE ( MIN ( dim_week[sort_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[fy] = _wfy )
VAR _ps         = IF ( _gr = "W", _wps, _s )
RETURN
    CALCULATE (
        VAR _h1        = CALCULATE ( [Mix Store %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h2        = CALCULATE ( [Mix Online %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h3        = CALCULATE ( [Mix Wholesale %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h4        = CALCULATE ( [Mix Store % Bud], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h5        = CALCULATE ( [Mix Online % Bud], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h6        = CALCULATE ( [Mix Wholesale % Bud], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h7        = CALCULATE ( [Mix Max Dev Channel], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h8        = CALCULATE ( [Mix Max Dev pp], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h9        = CALCULATE ( [Mix Store Net Sales], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h10       = CALCULATE ( [Mix Online Net Sales], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h11       = CALCULATE ( [Mix Wholesale Net Sales], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h12       = CALCULATE ( [Net Sales], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
        VAR _h13       = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _ls ), CALCULATE ( [Mix Store %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ls, dim_week[sort_index] <= _le ) )
        VAR _h14       = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _s2 ), CALCULATE ( [Mix Store %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s2, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e2 ) )
        VAR _h15       = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _ls ), CALCULATE ( [Mix Online %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ls, dim_week[sort_index] <= _le ) )
        VAR _h16       = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _s2 ), CALCULATE ( [Mix Online %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s2, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e2 ) )
        VAR _h17       = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _ls ), CALCULATE ( [Mix Wholesale %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ls, dim_week[sort_index] <= _le ) )
        VAR _h18       = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _s2 ), CALCULATE ( [Mix Wholesale %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s2, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e2 ) )
        VAR _v          = _h1
        VAR _b          = _h4
        VAR _mv1        = _h1
        VAR _mp1        = _h4
        VAR _mv2        = _h2
        VAR _mp2        = _h5
        VAR _mv3        = _h3
        VAR _mp3        = _h6
        VAR _mc1        = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", _h13, "LW", _h14, _mp1 )
        VAR _mc2        = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", _h15, "LW", _h16, _mp2 )
        VAR _mc3        = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", _h17, "LW", _h18, _mp3 )
        VAR _mq1        = _mv1 - _mc1
        VAR _mr1        = _mv1 - _mp1
        VAR _mq2        = _mv2 - _mc2
        VAR _mr2        = _mv2 - _mp2
        VAR _mq3        = _mv3 - _mc3
        VAR _mr3        = _mv3 - _mp3
        VAR _j          = SWITCH ( TRUE (), ABS ( _mq1 ) >= ABS ( _mq2 ) && ABS ( _mq1 ) >= ABS ( _mq3 ), 1, ABS ( _mq2 ) >= ABS ( _mq3 ), 2, 3 )
        VAR _dpp        = SWITCH ( _j, 1, _mq1, 2, _mq2, _mq3 )
        VAR _jp         = SWITCH ( TRUE (), ABS ( _mr1 ) >= ABS ( _mr2 ) && ABS ( _mr1 ) >= ABS ( _mr3 ), 1, ABS ( _mr2 ) >= ABS ( _mr3 ), 2, 3 )
        VAR _mpp        = SWITCH ( _jp, 1, _mr1, 2, _mr2, _mr3 )
        VAR _lab        = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", "vs LY", "LW", _pshort, "vs plan" )
        VAR _dt         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _mc1 ) || ISBLANK ( _mv1 ), "n/a " & _lab, SWITCH ( _j, 1, "store", 2, "online", "wholesale" ) & " " & ( VAR _f900003 = _dpp RETURN IF ( _f900003 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900003 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) & " " & _lab )
        VAR _bad        = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _mp1 ), ABS ( _mpp ) )
        VAR _sw         = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[watch_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "CHANNEL_MIX" )
        VAR _so         = LOOKUPVALUE ( param_status_rule[off_from], param_status_rule[rule_code], "CHANNEL_MIX" )
        VAR _rb         = ROUND ( _bad, 10 )
        VAR _st         = IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _bad ), SWITCH ( TRUE (), _rb <= _sw, 1, _rb <= _so, 2, 3 ) )
        VAR _val        = IF ( _cna, "–", IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ), "n/a", ( VAR _f900001 = _v RETURN IF ( _f900001 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900001 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) ) ) )
        VAR _unit       = IF ( ISBLANK ( _v ), "", "% store" )
        VAR _bt         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _b ), "n/a", ( VAR _f900002 = _b RETURN IF ( _f900002 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f900002 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) ) )
        VAR _dlong      = _dt
        VAR _sub        = "% of net sales vs plan" & IF ( LOOKUPVALUE ( dim_kpi[is_provisional], dim_kpi[kpi_code], "CHANNEL_MIX" ), " · prov.", "" )
        VAR _ink        = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#136C3A", 2, "#9A5B00", 3, "#C0272D", "#5F6878" )
        VAR _cur        = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#136C3A", 2, "#9A5B00", 3, "#C0272D", "#111827" )
        VAR _tint       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "#E1F0E5", 2, "#FBEFD6", 3, "#FAE3E3", "#F1F3F5" )
        VAR _chip       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "▲ On track", 2, "● Watch", 3, "▼ Off track", "– N/A" )
        VAR _glyph      = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "▲", 2, "●", 3, "▼", "–" )
        VAR _word       = SWITCH ( _st, 1, "On track", 2, "Watch", 3, "Off track", "N/A" )
        VAR _cw         = SWITCH ( _st, 1, 74.8, 2, 63.5, 3, 76.0, 51.2 )
        VAR _sl0        = "Store"
        VAR _sv0        = IF ( _cna && _sl0 <> "", "n/a", ( VAR _f313 = ( _h1 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( _f313 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f313 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) & " · " & IF ( ISBLANK ( _h4 ), "n/a", ( VAR _f314 = ( _h1 + 0 ) - _h4 RETURN IF ( _f314 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f314 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) ) )
        VAR _sl1        = "Online"
        VAR _sv1        = IF ( _cna && _sl1 <> "", "n/a", ( VAR _f315 = ( _h2 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( _f315 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f315 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) & " · " & IF ( ISBLANK ( _h5 ), "n/a", ( VAR _f316 = ( _h2 + 0 ) - _h5 RETURN IF ( _f316 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f316 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) ) )
        VAR _sl2        = "Wholesale"
        VAR _sv2        = IF ( _cna && _sl2 <> "", "n/a", ( VAR _f317 = ( _h3 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( _f317 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f317 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) & " · " & IF ( ISBLANK ( _h6 ), "n/a", ( VAR _f318 = ( _h3 + 0 ) - _h6 RETURN IF ( _f318 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f318 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) ) )
        VAR _ex         = "Store " & ( VAR _f306 = ( _h9 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f306 ), "n/a", IF ( _f306 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f306 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f306 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f306 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & ", online " & ( VAR _f307 = ( _h10 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f307 ), "n/a", IF ( _f307 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f307 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f307 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f307 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & ", wholesale " & ( VAR _f308 = ( _h11 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f308 ), "n/a", IF ( _f308 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f308 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f308 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f308 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & " of " & ( VAR _f309 = ( _h12 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( ISBLANK ( _f309 ), "n/a", IF ( _f309 < 0, "−", "" ) & "€" & IF ( ABS ( _f309 ) / 1000000 >= 1000, FORMAT ( ABS ( _f309 / 1000000 ), "#,0" ), FORMAT ( ABS ( _f309 / 1000000 ), "#,0.0" ) ) & "m" ) ) & " → " & "**" & SUBSTITUTE ( ( VAR _f310 = ( _h1 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( _f310 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f310 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) & " / " & ( VAR _f311 = ( _h2 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( _f311 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f311 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ) & " / " & ( VAR _f312 = ( _h3 + 0 ) RETURN IF ( _f312 < 0, "−", "" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f312 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & "%" ), " ", UNICHAR ( 160 ) ) & "**" & IF ( ISBLANK ( _h4 ), ".", ". Plan " & ( VAR _f299 = _h4 RETURN ( VAR _f300 = FORMAT ( ABS ( _f299 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) RETURN IF ( RIGHT ( _f300, 2 ) = ".0", LEFT ( _f300, LEN ( _f300 ) - 2 ), _f300 ) ) ) & " / " & ( VAR _f301 = _h5 RETURN ( VAR _f302 = FORMAT ( ABS ( _f301 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) RETURN IF ( RIGHT ( _f302, 2 ) = ".0", LEFT ( _f302, LEN ( _f302 ) - 2 ), _f302 ) ) ) & " / " & ( VAR _f303 = _h6 RETURN ( VAR _f304 = FORMAT ( ABS ( _f303 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) RETURN IF ( RIGHT ( _f304, 2 ) = ".0", LEFT ( _f304, LEN ( _f304 ) - 2 ), _f304 ) ) ) & ", so " & SWITCH ( _h7, 1, "store", 2, "online", "wholesale" ) & " " & ( VAR _f305 = _h8 RETURN IF ( _f305 >= 0, "+", "−" ) & FORMAT ( ABS ( _f305 * 100 ), "#,0.0" ) & " pp" ) & "." )
        VAR _footr      = [Updated Text]
        VAR _pk         =
            SUBSTITUTE ( "Explains Tier 1 · " & _sub, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _dlong, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl0, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv0, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl1, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sv1, "|", "/" )
                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( _sl2, "|", "/" )

                & "|" & SUBSTITUTE ( "Drill path · Channel & customer page", "|", "/" )

            _pk
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_region )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_granularity )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_comparator )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_group )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_lever )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( param_attr_owner )
        ,FILTER ( ALL ( dim_store[scope_code] ), _sc = "ALL" || dim_store[scope_code] = _sc )
    )
```

**Example Pre**

```dax
Example Pre =
VAR _x         = PATHITEM ( [Explain Pack], 9 )
VAR _p1        = FIND ( "**", _x, 1, 0 )
VAR _p2        = IF ( _p1 > 0, FIND ( "**", _x, _p1 + 2, 0 ), 0 )
RETURN
    IF ( _p1 = 0, _x, LEFT ( _x, _p1 - 1 ) )
```

**Example Bold**

```dax
Example Bold =
VAR _x         = PATHITEM ( [Explain Pack], 9 )
VAR _p1        = FIND ( "**", _x, 1, 0 )
VAR _p2        = IF ( _p1 > 0, FIND ( "**", _x, _p1 + 2, 0 ), 0 )
RETURN
    IF ( _p1 > 0 && _p2 > 0, SUBSTITUTE ( MID ( _x, _p1 + 2, _p2 - _p1 - 2 ), UNICHAR ( 160 ), " " ), "" )
```

**Example Post**

```dax
Example Post =
VAR _x         = PATHITEM ( [Explain Pack], 9 )
VAR _p1        = FIND ( "**", _x, 1, 0 )
VAR _p2        = IF ( _p1 > 0, FIND ( "**", _x, _p1 + 2, 0 ), 0 )
RETURN
    IF ( _p1 > 0 && _p2 > 0, MID ( _x, _p2 + 2, LEN ( _x ) ), "" )
```

</details>

## 9 Engine

Hidden helpers: picker codes, the picked item and its window bounds from dim_item, scoped leaf sums.

| Measure | Definition | Spec | Format | Hidden |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `_Scope Code` | Region / format switcher (E-15): ALL, NL, BE or ONLINE; ALL when nothing is picked. | E-15 | – | yes |
| `_Category Active` | Category pick active (E-100, E-101): the Category slicer filters dim_category and leaves fewer than all 9 categories. All 9 ticked behaves like no pick (ruling 2026-10-30), so ISFILTERED alone is not the guard. | E-100, E-101 | – | yes |
| `_Category Label` | Header segment of the category pick (E-103, js-exec CATEGORY_COPY): ' · Electronics', ' · Electronics & Toys & leisure' (menu order), ' · 3 categories' from 3 up; empty at All (nothing or all 9 picked). | E-103 | – | yes |
| `_Granularity Code` | Period type (E-90): W (Week), P (Month = 4-5-4 retail period), Q (Quarter) or FY (Year); W when nothing is picked. | E-90 | – | yes |
| `_Comparator Code` | Comparator switcher (E-14, E-94): BUD, LY or LW (= Previous item); BUD when nothing is picked. Previous is not offered at Year and reads LY there. Drives the delta row only (E-24). | E-14, E-24, E-94 | – | yes |
| `_Comparator Row Visible` | 1 when a comparator row is offered; 0 for Previous at Year (E-94). Visual-level filter of menu_param_comparator. | E-94 | 0 | yes |
| `_AsOf Index` | dim_week sort_index of the as-of week (manifest.json current_week_key, FY2026-W43 = 95, E-05). | E-05 | #,0 | yes |
| `_Item Key` | The picked item (E-91): the one dim_item row left by the type, FY and item slicers (Week / Month grid on label, Quarter / Year row on label_wide); otherwise the item of the picked type that holds the last week of the item last picked in the picked FY (or of that FY's last item, the as-of week in the as-of FY), so W43 -> P10 -> Q4 -> FY2026 and Reset lands on Week 43 FY2026 (js-exec menu(), E-90). | E-90, E-91 | – | yes |
| `_Window Start` | First sort_index of the picked item's window (item weeks within actuals, E-92). | E-92 | #,0 | yes |
| `_Window End` | Last sort_index of the picked item's window = L, the week point-in-time cards read (E-92, E-93). | E-92, E-93 | #,0 | yes |
| `_LY Window Start` | First sort_index of the LY window: the window's weeks one year back by ly_week_key (E-12, E-94); blank in FY2025. | E-12, E-94 | #,0 | yes |
| `_LY Window End` | Last sort_index of the LY window; blank in FY2025. | – | #,0 | yes |
| `_Prev Window Start` | First sort_index of the previous-item window (E-94): lw_week_key at Week; the item before at Month / Quarter / Year, cut to the picked item's week count when it is partial; blank for the first item. | E-94 | #,0 | yes |
| `_Prev Window End` | Last sort_index of the previous-item window. | – | #,0 | yes |
| `_EB Prev Start` | EBITDA flash, comparator Previous (E-93, ET-31): at Week the previous month's first k weeks (k = the week's position in its month; P1 -> previous FY P12; blank for week 5 of a 5-week month); else = _Prev Window Start. | E-93, ET-31 | #,0 | yes |
| `_EB Prev End` | Last sort_index of the EBITDA Previous window. | – | #,0 | yes |
| `_EB Prev Name` | Name of the EBITDA Previous window at Week for the i delta line, e.g. P9 weeks 1-4 (USER 2026-10-30). | – | – | yes |
| `_Item Sort` | Position of the picked item among the items of its type (1 = FY2025 W1 / P1 / Q1 / FY2025). | – | #,0 | yes |
| `_Prev Short` | Delta suffix of the Previous comparator, always short (E-98): vs prev. wk / mth / qtr (vs LY at Year). | E-98 | – | yes |
| `_Units` | Units sold. Scoped by the region switcher (dim_store.scope_code; ALL = no filter). | – | #,0 | yes |
| `_Opex` | OpEx amount, every scenario and cost type (filter on top). Scoped by the region switcher (dim_store.scope_code; ALL = no filter). | – | #,0.00 | yes |
| `_Store Count` | Distinct sites with sales rows. Scoped by the region switcher (dim_store.scope_code; ALL = no filter). | – | #,0 | yes |
| `_Inv On Hand Cost` | On-hand cost, summed over the weeks in context (never use over a window). Scoped by the region switcher (dim_store.scope_code; ALL = no filter). | – | #,0.00 | yes |
| `_Inv On Order Cost` | On-order cost. Scoped by the region switcher (dim_store.scope_code; ALL = no filter). | – | #,0.00 | yes |
| `_Inv On Hand Units` | On-hand units. Scoped by the region switcher (dim_store.scope_code; ALL = no filter). | – | #,0 | yes |
| `_Forecast COGS` | Forecast weekly COGS. Scoped by the region switcher (dim_store.scope_code; ALL = no filter). | – | #,0.00 | yes |
| `_Forecast x Cover Target` | Forecast COGS weighted by the category cover target. Scoped by the region switcher (dim_store.scope_code; ALL = no filter). | – | #,0.00 | yes |
| `_Ranged SKU Days` | Ranged top-500 SKU-days. Scoped by the region switcher (dim_store.scope_code; ALL = no filter). | – | #,0 | yes |
| `_In Stock SKU Days` | In-stock SKU-days (sales-weighted). Scoped by the region switcher (dim_store.scope_code; ALL = no filter). | – | #,0.0 | yes |
| `_Receipt Units` | Receipt units into selling locations. Scoped by the region switcher (dim_store.scope_code; ALL = no filter). | – | #,0 | yes |
| `Card Mix Dev Channel` | Channel mix delta vs the comparator mix, packed: channel x 1000 + signed share deviation (1 store, 2 online, 3 wholesale; first on a tie). | – | – | yes |
| `_Chip Fill Selected` | Filters menu chip fill colour of a picked item (selected state of the button slicers, cobalt accent). | – | – | yes |
| `_Chip Line Selected` | Filters menu chip outline colour of a picked item (selected state of the button slicers, cobalt accent). | – | – | yes |
| `_Chip Font Selected` | Filters menu chip text colour of a picked item (selected state of the button slicers, cobalt accent). | – | – | yes |

<details><summary>DAX</summary>

**_Scope Code**

```dax
_Scope Code =
SELECTEDVALUE ( param_region[scope_code], "ALL" )
```

**_Category Active**

```dax
_Category Active =
ISFILTERED ( dim_category[category_name] )
    && COUNTROWS ( VALUES ( dim_category[category_code] ) ) < COUNTROWS ( ALL ( dim_category[category_code] ) )
```

**_Category Label**

```dax
_Category Label =
VAR _n          = COUNTROWS ( VALUES ( dim_category[category_code] ) )
RETURN
    SWITCH (
        TRUE ()
        ,NOT [_Category Active]
            ,""
        ,_n = 1
            ," · " & SELECTEDVALUE ( dim_category[category_name] )
        ,_n = 2
            ," · " & CONCATENATEX ( VALUES ( dim_category[category_name] ), dim_category[category_name], " & ", CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_category[sort_order] ) ), ASC )
        ," · " & _n & " categories"
    )
```

**_Granularity Code**

```dax
_Granularity Code =
SELECTEDVALUE ( param_granularity[granularity_code], "W" )
```

**_Comparator Code**

```dax
_Comparator Code =
VAR _c          = SELECTEDVALUE ( param_comparator[comparator_code], "BUD" )
RETURN
    IF ( _c = "LW" && [_Granularity Code] = "FY", "LY", _c )
```

**_Comparator Row Visible**

```dax
_Comparator Row Visible =
IF ( SELECTEDVALUE ( param_comparator[comparator_code] ) = "LW" && [_Granularity Code] = "FY", 0, 1 )
```

**_AsOf Index**

```dax
_AsOf Index =
VAR _key        = MAX ( meta_dataset[current_week_key] )
RETURN
    CALCULATE (
        MAX ( dim_week[sort_index] )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week )
        ,dim_week[week_key] = _key
    )
```

**_Item Key**

```dax
_Item Key =
VAR _g          = [_Granularity Code]
VAR _one        = HASONEVALUE ( dim_item[item_key] )
VAR _hitk       = IF ( _one, SELECTEDVALUE ( dim_item[item_key] ) )
VAR _hitg       = IF ( _one, SELECTEDVALUE ( dim_item[granularity_code] ) )
VAR _a          = [_AsOf Index]
VAR _afy        = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_week[fy] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] = _a )
VAR _fyf        = IF ( ISFILTERED ( dim_item[fy_label] ), MAXX ( FILTERS ( dim_item[fy_label] ), dim_item[fy_label] ) )
VAR _fy         = IF ( ISBLANK ( _fyf ), _afy, VALUE ( RIGHT ( _fyf, 4 ) ) )
VAR _lab        =
    IF (
        _g IN { "Q", "FY" }
        ,IF ( ISFILTERED ( dim_item[label_wide] ), MAXX ( FILTERS ( dim_item[label_wide] ), dim_item[label_wide] ) )
        ,IF ( ISFILTERED ( dim_item[label] ), MAXX ( FILTERS ( dim_item[label] ), dim_item[label] ) )
    )
VAR _anchor0    =
    CALCULATE (
        MAX ( dim_item[end_index] )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item )
        ,dim_item[fy] = _fy
        ,dim_item[label] = _lab
    )
VAR _fyend      = CALCULATE ( MAX ( dim_item[end_index] ), REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item ), dim_item[fy] = _fy )
VAR _anchor     = IF ( ISBLANK ( _anchor0 ), _fyend, _anchor0 )
VAR _fallback   =
    CALCULATE (
        MAX ( dim_item[item_key] )
        ,REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_item )
        ,dim_item[granularity_code] = _g
        ,dim_item[start_index] <= _anchor
        ,dim_item[end_index] >= _anchor
    )
RETURN
    IF ( _hitg = _g, _hitk, _fallback )
```

**Card Mix Dev Channel**

```dax
Card Mix Dev Channel =
VAR _s          = [_Window Start]
VAR _e          = [_Window End]
VAR _ls         = [_LY Window Start]
VAR _le         = [_LY Window End]
VAR _s2         = [_Prev Window Start]
VAR _e2         = [_Prev Window End]
VAR _cmp        = [_Comparator Code]
VAR _v1         = CALCULATE ( [Mix Store %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
VAR _v2         = CALCULATE ( [Mix Online %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
VAR _v3         = CALCULATE ( [Mix Wholesale %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e )
VAR _c1         = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _ls ), CALCULATE ( [Mix Store %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ls, dim_week[sort_index] <= _le ) ), "LW", IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _s2 ), CALCULATE ( [Mix Store %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s2, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e2 ) ), CALCULATE ( [Mix Store % Bud], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e ) )
VAR _c2         = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _ls ), CALCULATE ( [Mix Online %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ls, dim_week[sort_index] <= _le ) ), "LW", IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _s2 ), CALCULATE ( [Mix Online %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s2, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e2 ) ), CALCULATE ( [Mix Online % Bud], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e ) )
VAR _c3         = SWITCH ( _cmp, "LY", IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _ls ), CALCULATE ( [Mix Wholesale %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _ls, dim_week[sort_index] <= _le ) ), "LW", IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _s2 ), CALCULATE ( [Mix Wholesale %], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s2, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e2 ) ), CALCULATE ( [Mix Wholesale % Bud], REMOVEFILTERS ( dim_week ), dim_week[sort_index] >= _s, dim_week[sort_index] <= _e ) )
VAR _d1         = _v1 - _c1
VAR _d2         = _v2 - _c2
VAR _d3         = _v3 - _c3
VAR _j          =
    SWITCH (
        TRUE ()
        ,ABS ( _d1 ) >= ABS ( _d2 ) && ABS ( _d1 ) >= ABS ( _d3 )
            ,1
        ,ABS ( _d2 ) >= ABS ( _d3 )
            ,2
        ,3
    )
RETURN
    IF ( NOT ISBLANK ( _c1 ) && NOT ISBLANK ( _v1 ), _j * 1000 + SWITCH ( _j, 1, _d1, 2, _d2, _d3 ) )
```
